+extension AppModel: CardCustomizationSaving {
+    func beginCardCustomization() -> CardCustomizationController? {
+        guard case .ready = launchState, let snapshot, snapshot.cardIssued,
+              let card = snapshot.currentCard else { return nil }
+        return CardCustomizationController(card: card, saver: self)
+    }
+
+    func saveCardStyle(_ style: CardStyle, forCardID id: String) -> Bool {
+        guard case .ready = launchState, let snapshot, snapshot.cardIssued,
+              let card = snapshot.currentCard, card.id == id else {
+            if case let .failed(issue) = launchState { saveFailure = .writeBlocked(issue) }
+            else { saveFailure = .notReady }
+            return false
+        }
+        let cards = CardSection(items: snapshot.cards.items.map {
+            $0.id == id ? $0.updating(style: style) : $0
+        })
+        return save(snapshot.updating(cards: cards))
+    }
+
+    var currentCardStyle: CardStyle { snapshot?.currentCard?.style ?? .standard }
+}

diff --git a/ios/FocusMate/CardCustomizationController.swift b/ios/FocusMate/CardCustomizationController.swift

+import Foundation
+import Observation
+
+struct CardPhotoInput {
+    let load: @MainActor () async throws -> Data?
+
+    init(load: @escaping @MainActor () async throws -> Data?) {
+        self.load = load
+    }
+}
+
+enum CardLayerDirection {
+    case forward, backward
+}
+
+enum CardCustomizationExitDecision: Equatable {
+    case dismiss, confirmDiscard
+}
+
+@MainActor
+protocol CardCustomizationSaving: AnyObject {
+    func saveCardStyle(_ style: CardStyle, forCardID id: String) -> Bool
+    var saveFailure: StorageIssue? { get }
+}
+
+enum CardCustomizationNotice: Equatable {
+    case maximumPhotos
+    case importFailed([CardPhotoProcessingIssue])
+    case partialImport(failures: [CardPhotoProcessingIssue], omittedCount: Int)
+    case saveFailed(StorageIssue)
+
+    var message: String {
+        func importMessage(_ base: String, failures: [CardPhotoProcessingIssue]) -> String {
+            let reasons = failures.reduce(into: [String]()) { reasons, issue in
+                let reason: String? = switch issue {
+                case .inputTooLarge: "선택한 입력 사진은 장당 8MiB 이하여야 해요."
+                case .unsupportedAspectRatio: "사진의 가로·세로 비율은 0.1~10 범위여야 해요."
+                case .cannotMeetStorageLimit: "사진을 1MiB 이하로 압축해 저장하지 못했어요."
+                case .encodingFailed: "사진 변환에 실패했어요."
+                case .unreadable: nil
+                }
+                if let reason, !reasons.contains(reason) { reasons.append(reason) }
+            }
+            return ([base] + reasons).joined(separator: " ")
+        }
+        func storageReason(_ issue: StorageIssue) -> String? {
+            switch issue {
+            case .invalidState(.invalidCardStyle(.photoTooLarge)):
+                "사진 한 장의 저장 용량이 1MiB를 초과했어요."
+            case .invalidState(.invalidCardStyle(.totalPhotosTooLarge)):
+                "사진 전체의 저장 용량이 6MiB를 초과했어요."
+            case let .writeBlocked(cause): storageReason(cause)
+            default: nil
+            }
+        }
+        switch self {
+        case .maximumPhotos:
+            return "사진은 최대 6장까지 넣을 수 있어요."
+        case let .importFailed(failures):
+            return importMessage("사진을 읽지 못했어요. 사진 추가로 다시 선택해주세요.", failures: failures)
+        case let .partialImport(failures, omittedCount):
+            let base = omittedCount > 0 ? "사진은 최대 6장까지 넣을 수 있어요. 일부 사진은 추가하지 않았어요." : "사진 \(failures.count)장을 읽지 못했어요. 사진 추가로 다시 선택해주세요."
+            return importMessage(base, failures: failures)
+        case let .saveFailed(issue):
+            let base = "카드 외형을 저장하지 못했어요. 초안은 유지돼요. 저장 버튼으로 다시 시도해주세요."
+            return storageReason(issue).map { base + " " + $0 } ?? base
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+@MainActor @Observable
+final class CardCustomizationController {
+    private(set) var draft: CardStyle
+    private(set) var selectedPhotoID: UUID?
+    private(set) var isImporting = false
+    private(set) var isSaving = false
+    private(set) var didSave = false
+    private(set) var notice: CardCustomizationNotice?
+
+    private var baseline: CardStyle
+    private var history: [CardStyle] = []
+    private var isActive = true
+    @ObservationIgnored private var generation = 0
+    @ObservationIgnored private let cardID: String
+    @ObservationIgnored private let saver: any CardCustomizationSaving
+    @ObservationIgnored private let processor: any CardPhotoProcessing
+    @ObservationIgnored private let makeID: () -> UUID
+
+    init(card: CardSnapshot, saver: any CardCustomizationSaving,
+         processor: any CardPhotoProcessing = ImageIOCardPhotoProcessor(),
+         makeID: @escaping () -> UUID = UUID.init) {
+        cardID = card.id
+        baseline = card.style
+        draft = card.style
+        self.saver = saver
+        self.processor = processor
+        self.makeID = makeID
+    }
+
+    var hasChanges: Bool { draft != baseline }
+    var canUndo: Bool { !history.isEmpty }
+    var canAddPhotos: Bool {
+        canMutate && draft.photos.count < CardCustomizationPolicy.maximumPhotos
+    }
+    private var canMutate: Bool { isActive && !isImporting && !isSaving }
+
+    func selectPhoto(_ id: UUID?) {
+        guard isActive, !isSaving else { return }
+        if let id, !draft.photos.contains(where: { $0.id == id }) { return }
+        selectedPhotoID = id
+    }
+
+    func setBackground(_ color: CardColor) {
+        guard canMutate,
+              [color.red, color.green, color.blue].allSatisfy({ $0.isFinite && (0...1).contains($0) }) else { return }
+        var next = draft
+        next.background = color
+        apply(next)
+    }
+
+    func setPlacement(photoID: UUID, centerX: Double, centerY: Double, widthFraction: Double) {
+        guard canMutate, centerX.isFinite, centerY.isFinite, widthFraction.isFinite,
+              let index = draft.photos.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == photoID }) else { return }
+        var next = draft
+        let centers = CardCustomizationPolicy.centerRange
+        let widths = CardCustomizationPolicy.widthFractionRange
+        next.photos[index].centerX = min(centers.upperBound, max(centers.lowerBound, centerX))
+        next.photos[index].centerY = min(centers.upperBound, max(centers.lowerBound, centerY))
+        next.photos[index].widthFraction = min(widths.upperBound, max(widths.lowerBound, widthFraction))
+        apply(next)
+    }
+
+    func moveLayer(photoID: UUID, direction: CardLayerDirection) {
+        guard canMutate, let index = draft.photos.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == photoID }) else { return }
+        let destination = index + (direction == .forward ? 1 : -1)
+        guard draft.photos.indices.contains(destination) else { return }
+        var next = draft
+        next.photos.swapAt(index, destination)
+        apply(next)
+    }
+
+    func deletePhoto(_ id: UUID) {
+        guard canMutate, let index = draft.photos.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == id }) else { return }
+        var next = draft
+        next.photos.remove(at: index)
+        apply(next)
+    }
+
+    func undo() {
+        guard canMutate, let previous = history.popLast() else { return }
+        draft = previous
+        clearMissingSelection()
+    }
+
+    func restoreDefault() {
+        guard canMutate else { return }
+        apply(.standard)
+    }
+
+    // A batch is committed once; failed inputs leave slots available to later inputs.
+    func importPhotos(_ inputs: [CardPhotoInput]) async {
+        guard canMutate, !inputs.isEmpty else { return }
+        let available = CardCustomizationPolicy.maximumPhotos - draft.photos.count
+        guard available > 0 else {
+            notice = .maximumPhotos
+            return
+        }
+        generation += 1
+        let token = generation
+        isImporting = true
+        defer {
+            if isCurrentImport(token) { isImporting = false }
+        }
+        var photos: [CardPhoto] = []
+        var failures: [CardPhotoProcessingIssue] = []
+        var omittedCount = 0
+        for input in inputs {
+            if photos.count == available {
+                omittedCount += 1
+                continue
+            }
+            let data: Data
+            do {
+                let loaded = try await input.load()
+                guard isCurrentImport(token) else { return }
+                guard let loaded else {
+                    failures.append(.unreadable)
+                    continue
+                }
+                data = loaded
+            } catch {
+                guard isCurrentImport(token) else { return }
+                failures.append(.unreadable)
+                continue
+            }
+            do {
+                let processed = try await processor.process(data)
+                guard isCurrentImport(token) else { return }
+                photos.append(CardPhoto(id: makeID(), data: processed.data,
+                                        pixelWidth: processed.pixelWidth, pixelHeight: processed.pixelHeight,
+                                        centerX: 0.5, centerY: 0.5, widthFraction: 0.6))
+            } catch {
+                guard isCurrentImport(token) else { return }
+                failures.append(error as? CardPhotoProcessingIssue ?? .unreadable)
+            }
+        }
+        guard isCurrentImport(token) else { return }
+        if let last = photos.last {
+            var next = draft
+            next.photos.append(contentsOf: photos)
+            apply(next)
+            selectedPhotoID = last.id
+            notice = failures.isEmpty && omittedCount == 0
+                ? nil : .partialImport(failures: failures, omittedCount: omittedCount)
+        } else {
+            notice = .importFailed(failures)
+        }
+    }
+
+    func requestExit() -> CardCustomizationExitDecision {
+        guard isActive else { return .dismiss }
+        return hasChanges || isImporting ? .confirmDiscard : .dismiss
+    }
+
+    func continueEditing() {}
+
+    func discard() {
+        guard isActive, !isSaving else { return }
+        generation += 1
+        isActive = false
+        draft = baseline
+        history.removeAll()
+        selectedPhotoID = nil
+        isImporting = false
+        isSaving = false
+        didSave = false
+        notice = nil
+    }
+
+    @discardableResult
+    func save() -> Bool {
+        guard canMutate else { return false }
+        isSaving = true
+        defer { isSaving = false }
+        guard saver.saveCardStyle(draft, forCardID: cardID) else {
+            notice = .saveFailed(saver.saveFailure ?? .writeFailed)
+            return false
+        }
+        baseline = draft
+        history.removeAll()
+        didSave = true
+        notice = nil
+        return true
+    }
+
+    private func isCurrentImport(_ token: Int) -> Bool {
+        isActive && generation == token
+    }
+
+    private func apply(_ next: CardStyle) {
+        guard next != draft else { return }
+        history.append(draft)
+        if history.count > CardCustomizationPolicy.undoLimit { history.removeFirst() }
+        draft = next
+        clearMissingSelection()
+    }
+
+    private func clearMissingSelection() {
+        if let selectedPhotoID, !draft.photos.contains(where: { $0.id == selectedPhotoID }) {
+            self.selectedPhotoID = nil
+        }
+    }
+}

+    let saved: () -> Void
+    @State private var pickerItems: [PhotosPickerItem] = []
+    @State private var confirmsExit = false

+    @State private var gestureTranslation: CGSize = .zero

+    private var selected: CardPhoto? { controller.draft.photos.first { $0.id == controller.selectedPhotoID } }

+            }), supportsOpacity: false)

+                Spacer()

+                             selectionBehavior: .ordered, matching: .images) {
+                    Label("사진 추가", systemImage: "plus").font(FontCatalog.text(12))
+                        .padding(.horizontal, 14).frame(minHeight: 43)
+                        .background(Color(red: 24 / 255, green: 29 / 255, blue: 32 / 255), in: RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 9))
+                        .overlay(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 9).stroke(secondary.opacity(0.5)))
+                }.disabled(!controller.canAddPhotos).accessibilityIdentifier("cardCustomization.photoPicker")
+            }
+            if controller.draft.photos.isEmpty {
+                Text("좋아하는 풍경, 소중한 순간을 담아보세요.").font(FontCatalog.text(12)).foregroundStyle(secondary)
+            } else {
+                ScrollView(.horizontal) {
+                    HStack(spacing: 10) {
+                        ForEach(Array(controller.draft.photos.enumerated()), id: \.element.id) { index, photo in
+                            Button { controller.selectPhoto(photo.id) } label: {
+                                if let image = UIImage(data: photo.data) {
+                                    Image(uiImage: image).resizable().scaledToFill().frame(width: 58, height: 58).clipped()
+                                        .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 10))
+                                        .overlay(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 10).stroke(controller.selectedPhotoID == photo.id ? .white : .clear, lineWidth: 2))
+                                }
+                            }.buttonStyle(.plain).disabled(locked)
+                                .accessibilityLabel("사진 \(index + 1)")
+                                .accessibilityAddTraits(controller.selectedPhotoID == photo.id ? .isSelected : [])
+                                .accessibilityIdentifier("cardCustomization.photo." + photo.id.uuidString)
+                        }
+                    }.padding(3)
+                }.accessibilityIdentifier("cardCustomization.photos")
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    private func tools(_ photo: CardPhoto) -> some View {
+        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 12) {
+            HStack { Text("사진 크기"); Spacer(); Text("\(Int(photo.widthFraction * 100))%") }
+            Slider(value: Binding(get: { selected?.widthFraction ?? photo.widthFraction }, set: { value in
+                if let p = selected { place(p, width: value) }
+            }), in: CardCustomizationPolicy.widthFractionRange)
+                .accessibilityLabel("사진 크기").accessibilityValue("\(Int(photo.widthFraction * 100))퍼센트")
+                .accessibilityIdentifier("cardCustomization.width")
+            positionControl(photo, axis: "x")
+            positionControl(photo, axis: "y")
+            ViewThatFits(in: .horizontal) {
+                HStack { layerButtons(photo) }
+                VStack(alignment: .leading) { layerButtons(photo) }
+            }
+        }.font(FontCatalog.text(15)).disabled(locked)
+    }
+
+    private func positionControl(_ photo: CardPhoto, axis: String) -> some View {
+        let x = axis == "x"
+        let value = x ? photo.centerX : photo.centerY
+        return VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 4) {
+            if dynamicTypeSize.isAccessibilitySize { Text(x ? "가로 위치" : "세로 위치") }
+            HStack {
+                if !dynamicTypeSize.isAccessibilitySize { Text(x ? "가로 위치" : "세로 위치") }
+                Spacer(minLength: 4)
+                Button { place(photo, x: x ? value - 0.05 : nil, y: x ? nil : value - 0.05) } label: { Image(systemName: "minus").frame(width: 44, height: 44) }
+                    .accessibilityLabel(x ? "가로 위치 줄이기" : "세로 위치 줄이기").accessibilityIdentifier("cardCustomization.\(axis).decrease")
+                    .disabled(value <= -1)
+                Text("\(Int((value * 100).rounded()))%").accessibilityValue("\(Int((value * 100).rounded()))퍼센트")
+                Button { place(photo, x: x ? value + 0.05 : nil, y: x ? nil : value + 0.05) } label: { Image(systemName: "plus").frame(width: 44, height: 44) }
+                    .accessibilityLabel(x ? "가로 위치 늘리기" : "세로 위치 늘리기").accessibilityIdentifier("cardCustomization.\(axis).increase")
+                    .disabled(value >= 2)
+            }
+        }.accessibilityElement(children: .contain).accessibilityIdentifier("cardCustomization." + axis)
+    }
+
+    @ViewBuilder private func layerButtons(_ photo: CardPhoto) -> some View {
+        Button("앞으로") { controller.moveLayer(photoID: photo.id, direction: .forward) }
+            .disabled(controller.draft.photos.last?.id == photo.id).accessibilityIdentifier("cardCustomization.forward").frame(minHeight: 44)
+        Button("뒤로") { controller.moveLayer(photoID: photo.id, direction: .backward) }
+            .disabled(controller.draft.photos.first?.id == photo.id).accessibilityIdentifier("cardCustomization.backward").frame(minHeight: 44)
+        Button("삭제", role: .destructive) { controller.deletePhoto(photo.id) }.accessibilityIdentifier("cardCustomization.delete").frame(minHeight: 44)
+    }
+
+    private var preview: some View {
+        GeometryReader { canvas in
+            let size = canvas.size
+            let style = previewStyle(size: size)
+            ZStack {
+                PassFace(style: style).accessibilityHidden(true)
+                if let photo = style.photos.first(where: { $0.id == controller.selectedPhotoID }) {
+                    let rect = photoRect(photo, size: size)
+                    Rectangle().stroke(.white, lineWidth: 1).frame(width: rect.width, height: rect.height)
+                        .overlay(alignment: .bottomTrailing) {
+                            Image(systemName: "arrow.up.left.and.arrow.down.right").font(.system(size: 12))
+                                .frame(width: 32, height: 32).background(.white, in: Circle()).foregroundStyle(.black)
+                        }.position(x: rect.midX, y: rect.midY).allowsHitTesting(false).accessibilityHidden(true)
+                }
+                // All gesture hit testing stays on this bounded canvas, even when a photo is enormous or outside it.
+                Color.clear.frame(width: size.width, height: size.height).contentShape(Rectangle())
+                    .gesture(DragGesture().onChanged { value in
+                        if gestureOrigin == nil {
+                            let point = value.startLocation
+                            if let selected, handleRect(selected, size: size).contains(point) {
+                                gestureOrigin = selected; resizing = true
+                            } else if let hit = controller.draft.photos.reversed().first(where: { photoRect($0, size: size).contains(point) }) {
+                                gestureOrigin = hit; resizing = false; controller.selectPhoto(hit.id)
+                            }
+                        }
+                        if gestureOrigin != nil { gestureTranslation = value.translation }
+                    }.onEnded { value in
+                        if let start = gestureOrigin {
+                            if resizing { place(start, width: start.widthFraction + value.translation.width / size.width) }
+                            else { place(start, x: start.centerX + value.translation.width / size.width, y: start.centerY + value.translation.height / size.height) }
+                        }
+                        gestureOrigin = nil; gestureTranslation = .zero
+                    }.exclusively(before: SpatialTapGesture().onEnded { value in
+                        let photo = controller.draft.photos.reversed().first { photoRect($0, size: size).contains(value.location) }
+                        controller.selectPhoto(photo?.id)
+                    }))
+                    .allowsHitTesting(!locked).accessibilityHidden(true)
+            }.frame(width: size.width, height: size.height)
+                .contentShape(Rectangle())
+                .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 16))
+                .accessibilityRepresentation {
+                    Color.clear.frame(width: size.width, height: size.height)
+                        .accessibilityElement().accessibilityLabel("카드 미리보기")
+                        .accessibilityIdentifier("cardCustomization.preview")
+                }
+        }.aspectRatio(PassFace.aspectRatio, contentMode: .fit)
+    }
+
+    private func photoRect(_ photo: CardPhoto, size: CGSize) -> CGRect {
+        let width = size.width * photo.widthFraction
+        let height = width * Double(photo.pixelHeight) / Double(photo.pixelWidth)
+        return CGRect(x: size.width * photo.centerX - width / 2, y: size.height * photo.centerY - height / 2, width: width, height: height)
+    }
+    private func handleRect(_ photo: CardPhoto, size: CGSize) -> CGRect {
+        let rect = photoRect(photo, size: size)
+        return CGRect(x: rect.maxX - 38, y: rect.maxY - 38, width: 44, height: 44)
+    }
+
+    private func previewStyle(size: CGSize) -> CardStyle {
+        var style = controller.draft
+        if let start = gestureOrigin, let index = style.photos.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == start.id }) {
+            if resizing {
+                style.photos[index].widthFraction = min(2.5, max(0.15, start.widthFraction + gestureTranslation.width / size.width))
+            } else {
+                style.photos[index].centerX = min(2, max(-1, start.centerX + gestureTranslation.width / size.width))
+                style.photos[index].centerY = min(2, max(-1, start.centerY + gestureTranslation.height / size.height))
+            }
+        }
+        return style
+    }
+
+    private func place(_ photo: CardPhoto, x: Double? = nil, y: Double? = nil, width: Double? = nil) {
+        guard let current = controller.draft.photos.first(where: { $0.id == photo.id }) else { return }
+        controller.setPlacement(photoID: photo.id, centerX: x ?? current.centerX, centerY: y ?? current.centerY, widthFraction: width ?? current.widthFraction)
+    }
+    private func startImport(_ inputs: [CardPhotoInput]) {
+        guard !locked else { return }
+        importTask = Task { await controller.importPhotos(inputs) }
+    }
+}

new file mode 100644

+import Foundation
+import ImageIO
+import UniformTypeIdentifiers
+import zlib
+
+struct ProcessedCardPhoto: Equatable, Sendable {
+    let data: Data
+    let pixelWidth: Int
+    let pixelHeight: Int
+
+    init(data: Data, pixelWidth: Int, pixelHeight: Int) {
+        self.data = data
+        self.pixelWidth = pixelWidth
+        self.pixelHeight = pixelHeight
+    }
+}
+
+enum CardPhotoProcessingIssue: Error, Equatable {
+    case inputTooLarge, unreadable, unsupportedAspectRatio
+    case encodingFailed, cannotMeetStorageLimit
+}
+
+protocol CardPhotoProcessing: Sendable {
+    func process(_ data: Data) async throws -> ProcessedCardPhoto
+}
+
+struct ImageIOCardPhotoProcessor: CardPhotoProcessing {
+    private static let maximumPixelSize = 2048
+    private static let minimumPixelSize = 64
+    private static let jpegQualities = [0.85, 0.7, 0.55, 0.4]
+
+    func process(_ data: Data) async throws -> ProcessedCardPhoto {
+        guard data.count <= CardCustomizationPolicy.inputByteLimit else { throw CardPhotoProcessingIssue.inputTooLarge }
+        let source = try Self.completeSource(data)
+        let dimensions = try Self.dimensions(source)
+        guard CardCustomizationPolicy.aspectRatioRange.contains(Double(dimensions.width) / Double(dimensions.height)) else {
+            throw CardPhotoProcessingIssue.unsupportedAspectRatio
+        }
+        var maximum = min(Self.maximumPixelSize, max(dimensions.width, dimensions.height))
+        while true {
+            let image = try Self.thumbnail(source, maximum: maximum)
+            let hasAlpha = Self.hasAlpha(image)
+            for quality in hasAlpha ? [1.0] : Self.jpegQualities {
+                let bytes = try Self.encode(image, hasAlpha: hasAlpha, quality: quality)
+                if bytes.count <= CardCustomizationPolicy.photoByteLimit {
+                    let outputSource = try Self.completeSource(bytes)
+                    let outputDimensions = try Self.dimensions(outputSource)
+                    guard outputDimensions.orientation == 1,
+                          max(outputDimensions.width, outputDimensions.height) <= Self.maximumPixelSize,
+                          CardCustomizationPolicy.aspectRatioRange.contains(Double(outputDimensions.width) / Double(outputDimensions.height)) else {
+                        throw CardPhotoProcessingIssue.encodingFailed
+                    }
+                    let decoded = try Self.thumbnail(outputSource, maximum: Self.maximumPixelSize)
+                    guard decoded.width == outputDimensions.width, decoded.height == outputDimensions.height else {
+                        throw CardPhotoProcessingIssue.encodingFailed
+                    }
+                    return ProcessedCardPhoto(data: bytes, pixelWidth: decoded.width, pixelHeight: decoded.height)
+                }
+            }
+            guard maximum > Self.minimumPixelSize else { throw CardPhotoProcessingIssue.cannotMeetStorageLimit }
+            maximum = max(Self.minimumPixelSize, Int(Double(maximum) * 0.75))
+        }
+    }
+
+    static func validateStoredPhoto(_ photo: CardPhoto) throws {
+        do {
+            guard !photo.data.isEmpty, photo.data.count <= CardCustomizationPolicy.photoByteLimit else {
+                throw SnapshotValidationIssue.invalidCardPhoto
+            }
+            let source = try completeSource(photo.data)
+            let size = try dimensions(source)
+            guard size.orientation == 1, size.width == photo.pixelWidth, size.height == photo.pixelHeight,
+                  max(size.width, size.height) <= maximumPixelSize,
+                  CardCustomizationPolicy.aspectRatioRange.contains(Double(size.width) / Double(size.height)) else {
+                throw SnapshotValidationIssue.invalidCardPhoto
+            }
+            let image = try thumbnail(source, maximum: maximumPixelSize)
+            guard image.width == photo.pixelWidth, image.height == photo.pixelHeight else {
+                throw SnapshotValidationIssue.invalidCardPhoto
+            }
+        } catch { throw SnapshotValidationIssue.invalidCardPhoto }
+    }
+
+    static func validateStoredStyle(_ style: CardStyle) throws {
+        for photo in style.photos { try validateStoredPhoto(photo) }
+    }
+
+    private static func completeSource(_ data: Data) throws -> CGImageSource {
+        guard !data.isEmpty,
+              let source = CGImageSourceCreateWithData(data as CFData, [kCGImageSourceShouldCache: false] as CFDictionary),
+              CGImageSourceGetCount(source) > 0,
+              CGImageSourceGetStatus(source) == .statusComplete,
+              CGImageSourceGetStatusAtIndex(source, 0) == .statusComplete else {
+            throw CardPhotoProcessingIssue.unreadable
+        }
+        if CGImageSourceGetType(source) == UTType.png.identifier as CFString {
+            try CardPhotoIntegrity.validatePNG(data)
+        }
+        try CardPhotoIntegrity.validateWebPPrefix(data)
+        return source
+    }
+
+    private static func dimensions(_ source: CGImageSource) throws -> (width: Int, height: Int, orientation: Int) {
+        guard let properties = CGImageSourceCopyPropertiesAtIndex(source, 0, nil) as? [CFString: Any],
+              let width = properties[kCGImagePropertyPixelWidth] as? Int,
+              let height = properties[kCGImagePropertyPixelHeight] as? Int, width > 0, height > 0 else {
+            throw CardPhotoProcessingIssue.unreadable
+        }
+        let orientation = properties[kCGImagePropertyOrientation] as? Int ?? 1
+        return (width: (5...8).contains(orientation) ? height : width,
+                height: (5...8).contains(orientation) ? width : height, orientation: orientation)
+    }
+
+    private static func hasAlpha(_ image: CGImage) -> Bool {
+        switch image.alphaInfo {
+        case .first, .last, .premultipliedFirst, .premultipliedLast, .alphaOnly: return true
+        default: return false
+        }
+    }
+
+    private static func thumbnail(_ source: CGImageSource, maximum: Int) throws -> CGImage {
+        let options: [CFString: Any] = [kCGImageSourceCreateThumbnailFromImageAlways: true,
+            kCGImageSourceThumbnailMaxPixelSize: maximum, kCGImageSourceCreateThumbnailWithTransform: true,
+            kCGImageSourceShouldCache: true, kCGImageSourceShouldCacheImmediately: true]
+        guard let image = CGImageSourceCreateThumbnailAtIndex(source, 0, options as CFDictionary),
+              image.width > 0, image.height > 0, max(image.width, image.height) <= maximum,
+              CGImageSourceGetStatus(source) == .statusComplete,
+              CGImageSourceGetStatusAtIndex(source, 0) == .statusComplete,
+              let context = CGContext(data: nil, width: image.width, height: image.height,
+                bitsPerComponent: 8, bytesPerRow: image.width * 4, space: image.colorSpace?.model == .rgb ? image.colorSpace! : CGColorSpaceCreateDeviceRGB(),
+                bitmapInfo: CGImageAlphaInfo.premultipliedLast.rawValue) else {
+            throw CardPhotoProcessingIssue.unreadable
+        }
+        context.draw(image, in: CGRect(x: 0, y: 0, width: image.width, height: image.height))
+        guard context.data != nil, context.makeImage() != nil,
+              CGImageSourceGetStatus(source) == .statusComplete,
+              CGImageSourceGetStatusAtIndex(source, 0) == .statusComplete else {
+            throw CardPhotoProcessingIssue.unreadable
+        }
+        return image
+    }
+
+    private static func encode(_ image: CGImage, hasAlpha: Bool, quality: Double) throws -> Data {
+        let data = NSMutableData()
+        let type = hasAlpha ? UTType.png : UTType.jpeg
+        guard let destination = CGImageDestinationCreateWithData(data, type.identifier as CFString, 1, nil) else {
+            throw CardPhotoProcessingIssue.encodingFailed
+        }
+        CGImageDestinationAddImage(destination, image,
+            [kCGImageDestinationLossyCompressionQuality: quality, kCGImagePropertyOrientation: 1] as CFDictionary)
+        guard CGImageDestinationFinalize(destination) else { throw CardPhotoProcessingIssue.encodingFailed }
+        return data as Data
+    }
+}
+
+
+// Integrity checks never materialize the original raster. ImageIO still owns pixel decoding.
+private enum CardPhotoIntegrity {
+    private static let inflateBufferSize = 64 * 1024
+
+    static func validatePNG(_ data: Data) throws {
+        try data.withUnsafeBytes { raw in
+            let bytes = raw.bindMemory(to: UInt8.self)
+            guard bytes.count >= 8, Array(bytes.prefix(8)) == [137, 80, 78, 71, 13, 10, 26, 10] else {
+                throw CardPhotoProcessingIssue.unreadable
+            }
+            var stream = z_stream()
+            guard inflateInit_(&stream, ZLIB_VERSION, Int32(MemoryLayout<z_stream>.size)) == Z_OK else {
+                throw CardPhotoProcessingIssue.unreadable
+            }
+            defer { inflateEnd(&stream) }
+            var output = [UInt8](repeating: 0, count: inflateBufferSize)
+            var rows: PNGScanlines?
+            var colorType: UInt8 = 0
+            var depth: UInt8 = 0
+            var paletteSeen = false
+            var idatSeen = false
+            var idatClosed = false
+            var streamEnded = false
+            var offset = 8
+            while offset <= bytes.count - 12 {
+                let length = Int(bigEndian32(bytes, offset))
+                guard length <= 0x7fff_ffff, length <= bytes.count - offset - 12 else {
+                    throw CardPhotoProcessingIssue.unreadable
+                }
+                let payload = offset + 8
+                let end = payload + length
+                let type = bigEndian32(bytes, offset + 4)
+                let checksum = crc32(0, bytes.baseAddress!.advanced(by: offset + 4), uInt(length + 4))
+                guard UInt32(checksum) == bigEndian32(bytes, end),
+                      bytes[(offset + 4)..<(offset + 8)].allSatisfy({ (65...90).contains($0) || (97...122).contains($0) }),
+                      bytes[offset + 6] & 0x20 == 0 else {
+                    throw CardPhotoProcessingIssue.unreadable
+                }
+                if rows == nil && type != 0x49484452 { throw CardPhotoProcessingIssue.unreadable }
+                if idatSeen && type != 0x49444154 { idatClosed = true }
+                switch type {
+                case 0x49484452: // IHDR
+                    guard rows == nil, offset == 8, length == 13 else { throw CardPhotoProcessingIssue.unreadable }
+                    let width = UInt64(bigEndian32(bytes, payload))
+                    let height = UInt64(bigEndian32(bytes, payload + 4))
+                    depth = bytes[payload + 8]
+                    colorType = bytes[payload + 9]
+                    let channels: UInt64
+                    let depths: [UInt8]
+                    switch colorType {
+                    case 0: channels = 1; depths = [1, 2, 4, 8, 16]
+                    case 2: channels = 3; depths = [8, 16]
+                    case 3: channels = 1; depths = [1, 2, 4, 8]
+                    case 4: channels = 2; depths = [8, 16]
+                    case 6: channels = 4; depths = [8, 16]
+                    default: throw CardPhotoProcessingIssue.unreadable
+                    }
+                    guard width > 0, height > 0, width <= 0x7fff_ffff, height <= 0x7fff_ffff,
+                          depths.contains(depth), bytes[payload + 10] == 0, bytes[payload + 11] == 0,
+                          bytes[payload + 12] <= 1 else { throw CardPhotoProcessingIssue.unreadable }
+                    rows = try PNGScanlines(width: width, height: height, bitsPerPixel: channels * UInt64(depth),
+                                            interlaced: bytes[payload + 12] == 1)
+                case 0x504c5445: // PLTE
+                    guard !paletteSeen, !idatSeen, colorType != 0, colorType != 4,
+                          length > 0, length % 3 == 0, length <= 768,
+                          colorType != 3 || length / 3 <= (1 << Int(depth)) else {
+                        throw CardPhotoProcessingIssue.unreadable
+                    }
+                    paletteSeen = true
+                case 0x49444154: // IDAT: one zlib stream across arbitrary chunk boundaries.
+                    guard !idatClosed, colorType != 3 || paletteSeen else { throw CardPhotoProcessingIssue.unreadable }
+                    idatSeen = true
+                    if !streamEnded {
+                        stream.next_in = UnsafeMutablePointer(mutating: bytes.baseAddress!.advanced(by: payload))
+                        stream.avail_in = uInt(length)
+                        try output.withUnsafeMutableBufferPointer { buffer in
+                            repeat {
+                                let previousInput = stream.avail_in
+                                stream.next_out = buffer.baseAddress!
+                                stream.avail_out = uInt(buffer.count)
+                                let status = inflate(&stream, Z_NO_FLUSH)
+                                let produced = buffer.count - Int(stream.avail_out)
+                                try rows!.consume(UnsafeBufferPointer(start: buffer.baseAddress, count: produced))
+                                if status == Z_STREAM_END {
+                                    guard rows!.isComplete else { throw CardPhotoProcessingIssue.unreadable }
+                                    streamEnded = true
+                                    break // PNG permits unused compressed bytes after the zlib end.
+                                }
+                                guard status == Z_OK || status == Z_BUF_ERROR else { throw CardPhotoProcessingIssue.unreadable }
+                                if produced == 0 && previousInput == stream.avail_in {
+                                    guard stream.avail_in == 0 else { throw CardPhotoProcessingIssue.unreadable }
+                                    break
+                                }
+                            } while stream.avail_in > 0 || stream.avail_out == 0
+                        }
+                    }
+                case 0x49454e44: // IEND
+                    guard length == 0, end + 4 == bytes.count, idatSeen, streamEnded, rows!.isComplete else {
+                        throw CardPhotoProcessingIssue.unreadable
+                    }
+                    return
+                default:
+                    guard bytes[offset + 4] & 0x20 != 0 else { throw CardPhotoProcessingIssue.unreadable }
+                }
+                offset = end + 4
+            }
+            throw CardPhotoProcessingIssue.unreadable
+        }
+    }
+
+    private struct PNGScanlines {
+        private var passes: [(bytes: UInt64, rows: UInt64)] = []
+        private var pass = 0
+        private var remainingInRow: UInt64 = 0
+        private var remainingTotal: UInt64 = 0
+
+        init(width: UInt64, height: UInt64, bitsPerPixel: UInt64, interlaced: Bool) throws {
+            let patterns: [(UInt64, UInt64, UInt64, UInt64)] = interlaced
+                ? [(0, 0, 8, 8), (4, 0, 8, 8), (0, 4, 4, 8), (2, 0, 4, 4),
+                   (0, 2, 2, 4), (1, 0, 2, 2), (0, 1, 1, 2)] : [(0, 0, 1, 1)]
+            for (x, y, dx, dy) in patterns where width > x && height > y {
+                let columns = (width - x + dx - 1) / dx
+                let rowCount = (height - y + dy - 1) / dy
+                let rowBytes = (columns * bitsPerPixel + 7) / 8
+                let size = (rowBytes + 1).multipliedReportingOverflow(by: rowCount)
+                let total = remainingTotal.addingReportingOverflow(size.partialValue)
+                guard !size.overflow, !total.overflow else { throw CardPhotoProcessingIssue.unreadable }
+                remainingTotal = total.partialValue
+                passes.append((rowBytes, rowCount))
+            }
+        }
+
+        var isComplete: Bool { remainingTotal == 0 }
+
+        mutating func consume(_ bytes: UnsafeBufferPointer<UInt8>) throws {
+            guard UInt64(bytes.count) <= remainingTotal else { throw CardPhotoProcessingIssue.unreadable }
+            var offset = 0
+            while offset < bytes.count {
+                if remainingInRow == 0 {
+                    while pass < passes.count && passes[pass].rows == 0 { pass += 1 }
+                    guard pass < passes.count, bytes[offset] <= 4 else { throw CardPhotoProcessingIssue.unreadable }
+                    passes[pass].rows -= 1
+                    remainingInRow = passes[pass].bytes
+                    offset += 1
+                }
+                let skipped = min(remainingInRow, UInt64(bytes.count - offset))
+                remainingInRow -= skipped
+                offset += Int(skipped)
+            }
+            remainingTotal -= UInt64(bytes.count)
+        }
+    }
+
+    // This is a required-prefix check, not a strict decoder for the entire VP8L body.
+    // Google WebP lossless specification: the first transform's fixed fields precede its image data.
+    static func validateWebPPrefix(_ data: Data) throws {
+        try data.withUnsafeBytes { raw in
+            let bytes = raw.bindMemory(to: UInt8.self)
+            guard bytes.count >= 12, bigEndian32(bytes, 0) == 0x52494646,
+                  bigEndian32(bytes, 8) == 0x57454250 else { return }
+            let riffEnd = Int(littleEndian32(bytes, 4)) + 8
+            guard riffEnd >= 12, riffEnd <= bytes.count else { throw CardPhotoProcessingIssue.unreadable }
+            var offset = 12
+            while offset <= riffEnd - 8 {
+                let length = Int(littleEndian32(bytes, offset + 4))
+                let payload = offset + 8
+                guard length <= riffEnd - payload, length + (length & 1) <= riffEnd - payload else {
+                    throw CardPhotoProcessingIssue.unreadable
+                }
+                if bigEndian32(bytes, offset) == 0x5650384c { // VP8L
+                    guard length >= 5, bytes[payload] == 0x2f, bytes[payload + 4] >> 5 == 0 else {
+                        throw CardPhotoProcessingIssue.unreadable
+                    }
+                    var bits = LosslessPrefixBits(bytes: bytes, bit: (payload + 5) * 8, end: (payload + length) * 8)
+                    if try bits.read(1) == 1 {
+                        switch try bits.read(2) {
+                        case 0, 1: _ = try bits.read(3) // block-size bits before the transform image
+                        case 2: _ = try bits.read(1) // subtract-green has no data; next transform flag is required
+                        case 3: _ = try bits.read(8) // color_table_size - 1, before the palette image
+                        default: break
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+                offset = payload + length + (length & 1)
+            }
+            guard offset == riffEnd else { throw CardPhotoProcessingIssue.unreadable }
+        }
+    }
+
+    private struct LosslessPrefixBits {
+        let bytes: UnsafeBufferPointer<UInt8>
+        var bit: Int
+        let end: Int
+
+        mutating func read(_ count: Int) throws -> UInt8 {
+            guard count <= end - bit else { throw CardPhotoProcessingIssue.unreadable }
+            var value: UInt8 = 0
+            for shift in 0..<count {
+                value |= ((bytes[bit / 8] >> (bit % 8)) & 1) << shift
+                bit += 1
+            }
+            return value
+        }
+    }
+
+    private static func bigEndian32(_ bytes: UnsafeBufferPointer<UInt8>, _ offset: Int) -> UInt32 {
+        (UInt32(bytes[offset]) << 24) | (UInt32(bytes[offset + 1]) << 16)
+            | (UInt32(bytes[offset + 2]) << 8) | UInt32(bytes[offset + 3])
+    }
+
+    private static func littleEndian32(_ bytes: UnsafeBufferPointer<UInt8>, _ offset: Int) -> UInt32 {
+        UInt32(bytes[offset]) | (UInt32(bytes[offset + 1]) << 8)
+            | (UInt32(bytes[offset + 2]) << 16) | (UInt32(bytes[offset + 3]) << 24)
+    }
+}

diff --git a/ios/FocusMate/CardStyle.swift b/ios/FocusMate/CardStyle.swift
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..f7e1c9c
--- /dev/null
+++ b/ios/FocusMate/CardStyle.swift
@@ -0,0 +1,96 @@
+import Foundation
+
+enum CardCustomizationPolicy {
+    static let inputByteLimit = 8_388_608
+    static let maximumPhotos = 6
+    static let photoByteLimit = 1_048_576
+    static let totalPhotoByteLimit = 6_291_456
+    static let aspectRatioRange = 0.1...10.0
+    static let widthFractionRange = 0.15...2.5
+    static let centerRange = -1.0...2.0
+    static let undoLimit = 20
+}
+
+struct CardColor: Codable, Equatable, Sendable {
+    let red: Double
+    let green: Double
+    let blue: Double
+    static let standard = CardColor(red: 18.0 / 255.0, green: 20.0 / 255.0, blue: 22.0 / 255.0)
+
+    init(red: Double, green: Double, blue: Double) {
+        self.red = red
+        self.green = green
+        self.blue = blue
+    }
+}
+
+struct CardPhoto: Codable, Equatable, Identifiable, Sendable {
+    let id: UUID
+    let data: Data
+    let pixelWidth: Int
+    let pixelHeight: Int
+    var centerX: Double
+    var centerY: Double
+    var widthFraction: Double
+
+    init(id: UUID, data: Data, pixelWidth: Int, pixelHeight: Int,
+         centerX: Double, centerY: Double, widthFraction: Double) {
+        self.id = id
+        self.data = data
+        self.pixelWidth = pixelWidth
+        self.pixelHeight = pixelHeight
+        self.centerX = centerX
+        self.centerY = centerY
+        self.widthFraction = widthFraction
+    }
+}
+
+enum CardStyleValidationIssue: Error, Equatable {
+    case invalidColor, tooManyPhotos(Int), duplicatePhotoID
+    case emptyPhotoData(UUID), invalidDimensions(UUID), invalidPlacement(UUID)
+    case photoTooLarge(UUID), totalPhotosTooLarge
+}
+
+struct CardStyle: Codable, Equatable, Sendable {
+    var background: CardColor
+    var photos: [CardPhoto]
+    static let standard = CardStyle(background: .standard, photos: [])
+
+    var isStandard: Bool { background == .standard && photos.isEmpty }
+
+    init(background: CardColor, photos: [CardPhoto]) {
+        self.background = background
+        self.photos = photos
+    }
+
+    func validate() throws {
+        guard [background.red, background.green, background.blue].allSatisfy({ $0.isFinite && (0...1).contains($0) }) else {
+            throw CardStyleValidationIssue.invalidColor
+        }
+        guard photos.count <= CardCustomizationPolicy.maximumPhotos else {
+            throw CardStyleValidationIssue.tooManyPhotos(photos.count)
+        }
+        guard Set(photos.map(\.id)).count == photos.count else { throw CardStyleValidationIssue.duplicatePhotoID }
+        var total = 0
+        for photo in photos {
+            guard photo.data.count <= CardCustomizationPolicy.totalPhotoByteLimit - total else {
+                throw CardStyleValidationIssue.totalPhotosTooLarge
+            }
+            total += photo.data.count
+        }
+        for photo in photos {
+            guard !photo.data.isEmpty else { throw CardStyleValidationIssue.emptyPhotoData(photo.id) }
+            guard photo.data.count <= CardCustomizationPolicy.photoByteLimit else { throw CardStyleValidationIssue.photoTooLarge(photo.id) }
+            guard photo.pixelWidth > 0, photo.pixelHeight > 0,
+                  CardCustomizationPolicy.aspectRatioRange.contains(Double(photo.pixelWidth) / Double(photo.pixelHeight)) else {
+                throw CardStyleValidationIssue.invalidDimensions(photo.id)
+            }
+            guard photo.centerX.isFinite, photo.centerY.isFinite, photo.widthFraction.isFinite,
+                  CardCustomizationPolicy.centerRange.contains(photo.centerX),
+                  CardCustomizationPolicy.centerRange.contains(photo.centerY),
+                  CardCustomizationPolicy.widthFractionRange.contains(photo.widthFraction) else {
+                throw CardStyleValidationIssue.invalidPlacement(photo.id)
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}

+import SwiftUI
+import CoreText
+
+/// Bundled Seoul city fonts; system text remains the fallback if registration fails.
+enum FontCatalog {
+    static func register() {
+        for name in ["SeoulAlrim-Medium", "SeoulAlrim-Bold"] {
+            if let url = Bundle.main.url(forResource: name, withExtension: "ttf") {
+                CTFontManagerRegisterFontsForURL(url as CFURL, .process, nil)
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    static func text(_ size: CGFloat, bold: Bool = false, relativeTo: Font.TextStyle = .body) -> Font {
+        .custom(bold ? "SeoulAlrimTTF-Bold" : "SeoulAlrimTTF-Medium", size: size, relativeTo: relativeTo)
+    }
+}

+    var swiftUIColor: Color { Color(.sRGB, red: red, green: green, blue: blue, opacity: 1) }

+/// v2 카드에서는 이후 추가된 style 키를 해석하지 않는다.
+struct LegacyCardV2DTO: Decodable {
+    let id: String
+    let issuedAt: Date
+    private enum CodingKeys: String, CodingKey { case id, issuedAt }
+    init(from decoder: Decoder) throws {
+        let c = try decoder.container(keyedBy: CodingKeys.self)
+        id = try c.decode(String.self, forKey: .id)
+        let text = try c.decode(String.self, forKey: .issuedAt)
+        guard let date = try? Date(text, strategy: .iso8601) else {
+            throw DecodingError.dataCorruptedError(forKey: .issuedAt, in: c, debugDescription: "issuedAt is not ISO 8601")
+        }
+        issuedAt = date
+    }
+}
+
+struct LegacySnapshotV2: Decodable {
+    struct Cards: Decodable { let items: [LegacyCardV2DTO] }
+    let cardIssued: Bool
+    let initialStation: StationSnapshot?
+    let currentStation: StationSnapshot?
+    let cards: Cards
+    let journey: JourneySection
+    let records: RecordSection
+}
+

@@ -23,6 +50,7 @@ enum SnapshotCodec {

+    static func migrateV2(_ legacy: LegacySnapshotV2) -> AppSnapshot {
+        AppSnapshot.empty.updatingStage(cardIssued: legacy.cardIssued,
+                                        initialStation: legacy.initialStation,
+                                        currentStation: legacy.currentStation)
+            .updating(cards: CardSection(items: legacy.cards.items.map { CardSnapshot(id: $0.id, issuedAt: $0.issuedAt) }))
+            .updating(journey: legacy.journey)
+            .updating(records: legacy.records)
+    }

+    @MainActor func cardPhotoInputs(gate: UITestCardImportGate?) -> [CardPhotoInput] {
+        guard let manifest = photoManifestURL else { return [] }
+        struct Manifest: Decodable { struct Item: Decodable { let path: String }; let items: [Item] }
+        guard let bytes = try? Data(contentsOf: manifest), let entries = try? JSONDecoder().decode(Manifest.self, from: bytes) else {
+            return [CardPhotoInput { throw CardPhotoProcessingIssue.unreadable }]
+        }
+        let token = gate?.arm()
+        let directory = manifest.deletingLastPathComponent().standardizedFileURL.resolvingSymlinksInPath()
+        return entries.items.enumerated().map { index, item in
+            CardPhotoInput {
+                if index == 0, let gate, let token { try await gate.wait(token) }
+                try Task.checkCancellation()
+                let lexical = directory.appendingPathComponent(item.path).standardizedFileURL
+                let resolved = lexical.resolvingSymlinksInPath()
+                guard !item.path.hasPrefix("/"), lexical.path.hasPrefix(directory.path + "/"),
+                      resolved.path.hasPrefix(directory.path + "/") else { throw CardPhotoProcessingIssue.unreadable }
+                return try Data(contentsOf: resolved)
+            }
+        }
+    }

+    @MainActor func cardStyleJSON(model: AppModel, controller: CardCustomizationController?) -> String {

+        func photos(_ style: CardStyle) -> [[String: Any]] {

+                "centerX": $0.centerX, "centerY": $0.centerY, "widthFraction": $0.widthFraction,
+                "byteCount": $0.data.count, "sha256": SHA256.hash(data: $0.data).map { String(format: "%02x", $0) }.joined()] }
+        }
+        let saved = model.currentCardStyle
+        let json: [String: Any] = ["cardID": model.snapshot?.currentCard?.id ?? "", "background": color(saved.background),
+            "photos": photos(saved), "draft": controller.map { ["background": color($0.draft.background), "photos": photos($0.draft)] as [String: Any] } ?? NSNull(),
+            "selectedPhotoID": controller?.selectedPhotoID?.uuidString as Any? ?? NSNull(),
+            "isImporting": controller?.isImporting ?? false, "canUndo": controller?.canUndo ?? false, "didSave": controller?.didSave ?? false]
+        guard let data = try? JSONSerialization.data(withJSONObject: json, options: .sortedKeys) else { return "null" }
+        return String(decoding: data, as: UTF8.self)
+    }

 }
+/// Only the editor Save action arms this shared box; bootstrap and unrelated saves never arm it.
+final class UITestCardSaveFailure {
+    private let enabled: Bool
+    private var used = false
+    private var armed = false
+    init(enabled: Bool) { self.enabled = enabled }
+    func arm() { if enabled && !used { armed = true } }
+    func consume() -> Bool {
+        guard armed else { return false }
+        armed = false; used = true
+        return true
+    }
+}
+
+@MainActor
+final class UITestCardImportGate {
+    private var token = UUID()
+    private var waiter: CheckedContinuation<Void, Error>?
+    private var released = false
+    func arm() -> UUID {
+        cancel(token)
+        token = UUID(); released = false
+        return token
+    }
+    func wait(_ expected: UUID) async throws {
+        try Task.checkCancellation()
+        guard expected == token else { throw CancellationError() }
+        if released { return }
+        try await withTaskCancellationHandler {
+            try await withCheckedThrowingContinuation { waiter = $0 }
+        } onCancel: {
+            Task { @MainActor in self.cancel(expected) }
+        }
+    }
+    func release() {
+        released = true
+        let continuation = waiter; waiter = nil
+        continuation?.resume()
+    }
+    private func cancel(_ expected: UUID) {
+        guard expected == token else { return }
+        let continuation = waiter; waiter = nil
+        continuation?.resume(throwing: CancellationError())
+    }
+}
 #endif

+            let bytes = Data(#"{"version":1,"state":{"cardIssued":true,"initialStation":null,"currentStation":null}}"#.utf8)
+            try CardPhotoTestFixtures.seed(bytes, at: url)
+            let model = loadedModel(url)
+            XCTAssertEqual(model.launchState, .ready(.initialStationEntry))
+            XCTAssertNil(model.snapshot?.currentCard)
+            // When / Then
+            XCTAssertNil(model.beginCardCustomization())
+            XCTAssertFalse(model.saveCardStyle(.standard, forCardID: Self.cardID))
+            XCTAssertEqual(model.saveFailure, .notReady)
+            XCTAssertEqual(model.currentCardStyle, .standard)
+            XCTAssertEqual(try Data(contentsOf: url), bytes)
+        }
+    }

+    // 깨짐 명명: 전달 CardID 검사를 제거하면 다른 카드용 외형이 저장되어 실패.
+    func testID불일치는_notReady이고_기존카드를_유지한다() throws {

+    // 깨짐 명명: 진입 snapshot을 저장에 재사용하면 이후 정류장/메모 변경이 소실되어 실패.
+    func testAC30_진입후_바뀐_최신정류장과_기록을_외형저장이_보존한다() throws {
+        try withTemporaryFile { url in

+                initialStation: StationSnapshot(id: "seongsu", name: "성수", direction: 1),
+                currentStation: StationSnapshot(id: "seongsu", name: "성수역", direction: -1))

+        }

+    // 깨짐 명명: 카드 외형 저장이 journeyPlan 또는 preparedJourney를 초기화하면 실행 중 준비 선택이 소실되어 실패.
+    func testAC30_외형저장은_메모리preparedJourney선택을_그대로유지한다() throws {

+            controller.setBackground(Self.editedColor)
+            XCTAssertTrue(controller.save())
+

+    func testAC24_실제파일_저장후_새모델은_여섯사진과_색_배치_순서를_복원한다() throws {

+    func test진입후_원본이_손상되면_저장은_writeBlocked이고_초안을_유지한다() throws {

+            try corrupt.write(to: url)
+            // When
+            XCTAssertFalse(controller.save())
+            // Then
+            XCTAssertEqual(model.saveFailure, .writeBlocked(.corrupt))
+            XCTAssertEqual(controller.notice, .saveFailed(.writeBlocked(.corrupt)))
+            XCTAssertEqual(controller.draft, CardStyle(background: Self.editedColor, photos: []))
+            XCTAssertFalse(controller.didSave)

+    // 깨짐 명명: 쓰기 실패 때 baseline/selection/history를 지우거나 재시도를 막으면 실패.
+    func testAC26_실제쓰기실패는_초안과_원본을_유지하고_재시도후에만_복원된다() throws {
+        try withTemporaryFile { url in
+            // Given: real LocalSnapshotStore; only write boundary throws once.
+            let original = CardPhotoTestFixtures.style([CardPhotoTestFixtures.photo(data: try CardPhotoTestFixtures.image(alpha: true))])
+            try seed(url, style: original)
+            let bytes = try Data(contentsOf: url)
+            var writes = 0
+            let store = LocalSnapshotStore(fileURL: url, writeData: { data, target in
+                writes += 1
+                if writes == 1 { throw ProbeError.write }
+                try data.write(to: target, options: .atomic)
+            })
+            let model = AppModel(store: store)
+            model.loadIfNeeded()
+            let before = model.snapshot
+            let controller = try XCTUnwrap(model.beginCardCustomization())
+            controller.selectPhoto(CardPhotoTestFixtures.photoID)
+            controller.setBackground(CardColor(red: 0.6, green: 0.5, blue: 0.4))
+            let expected = CardStyle(background: CardColor(red: 0.6, green: 0.5, blue: 0.4), photos: original.photos)
+            // When: first save fails.
+            XCTAssertFalse(controller.save())
+            // Then
+            XCTAssertEqual(writes, 1)
+            XCTAssertEqual(model.saveFailure, .writeFailed)
+            XCTAssertEqual(controller.notice, .saveFailed(.writeFailed))
+            XCTAssertEqual(controller.draft, expected)
+            XCTAssertEqual(controller.selectedPhotoID, UUID(uuidString: "11111111-2222-3333-4444-555555555555"))
+            XCTAssertTrue(controller.canUndo)
+            XCTAssertTrue(controller.hasChanges)
+            XCTAssertFalse(controller.didSave)
+            XCTAssertEqual(model.snapshot, before)
+            XCTAssertEqual(model.currentCardStyle, original)
+            XCTAssertEqual(try Data(contentsOf: url), bytes)
+            XCTAssertEqual(try LocalSnapshotStore(fileURL: url).load(), before)
+            // When: retry performs an actual atomic write, without reseeding.
+            XCTAssertTrue(controller.save())
+            // Then
+            XCTAssertEqual(writes, 2)
+            XCTAssertNil(model.saveFailure)
+            XCTAssertNil(controller.notice)
+            XCTAssertTrue(controller.didSave)
+            XCTAssertFalse(controller.canUndo)
+            XCTAssertFalse(controller.hasChanges)
+            let restarted = loadedModel(url)
+            XCTAssertEqual(restarted.currentCardStyle, expected)
+            assertIdentity(restarted.snapshot)
+            assertOriginalUsage(restarted.snapshot)
+            XCTAssertEqual(try XCTUnwrap(restarted.currentCardStyle.photos.first).data, original.photos[0].data)
+        }
+    }
+
+    // 깨짐 명명: begin이 v2를 쓰거나 saveCardStyle가 v3 변환/이용상태를 잃으면 실패.
+    func testAC29_v2진입은_쓰기없고_외형저장때만_v3로_전환한다() throws {
+        try withTemporaryFile { url in
+            // Given: hand-authored legacy envelope, independent of encoder.

+            let model = AppModel(store: LocalSnapshotStore(fileURL: url))
+            model.start()
+            let controller = try XCTUnwrap(model.beginCardCustomization())
+            XCTAssertEqual(controller.draft, .standard)
+            XCTAssertEqual(try Data(contentsOf: url), Self.legacyV2)
+            // When
+            controller.setBackground(Self.editedColor)
+            XCTAssertTrue(controller.save())
+            // Then: no seed on relaunch.
+            let root = try XCTUnwrap(JSONSerialization.jsonObject(with: Data(contentsOf: url)) as? [String: Any])
+            XCTAssertEqual(root["version"] as? Int, 3)
+            let restarted = loadedModel(url)
+            XCTAssertEqual(restarted.currentCardStyle, CardStyle(background: Self.editedColor, photos: []))
+            assertIdentity(restarted.snapshot)
+            assertOriginalUsage(restarted.snapshot)
+        }
+    }

+import Foundation
+@testable import FocusMate
+
+@MainActor
+final class DraftCardSaver: CardCustomizationSaving {
+    struct Call { let style: CardStyle; let cardID: String }
+    var calls: [Call] = []
+    var result = true
+    var saveFailure: StorageIssue?
+    var onSave: (@MainActor () -> Void)?
+    func saveCardStyle(_ style: CardStyle, forCardID id: String) -> Bool {
+        calls.append(Call(style: style, cardID: id))
+        onSave?()
+        return result
+    }
+}
+
+// Immutable Sendable boundary double: outcomes are fixtures, not controller state.
+struct DraftPhotoProcessor: CardPhotoProcessing {
+    func process(_ data: Data) async throws -> ProcessedCardPhoto {
+        switch data.first {
+        case 90: throw CardPhotoProcessingIssue.encodingFailed
+        case 91: throw CardPhotoProcessingIssue.unsupportedAspectRatio
+        default: return ProcessedCardPhoto(data: data, pixelWidth: 40, pixelHeight: 20)
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Explicit start signal and release. Cancellation deliberately does not release:
+// the late-return generation tests must exercise a boundary ignoring cancellation.
+actor DraftGate<Value: Sendable> {
+    private var started = false
+    private var startWaiters: [CheckedContinuation<Void, Never>] = []
+    private var pending: CheckedContinuation<Value, Error>?
+    func run() async throws -> Value {
+        try await withCheckedThrowingContinuation { continuation in
+            pending = continuation
+            started = true
+            let waiters = startWaiters
+            startWaiters.removeAll()
+            waiters.forEach { $0.resume() }
+        }
+    }
+    func waitForStart() async {
+        if started { return }
+        await withCheckedContinuation { startWaiters.append($0) }
+    }
+    func release(_ result: Result<Value, Error>) {
+        precondition(pending != nil, "release only after start")
+        let continuation = pending
+        pending = nil
+        continuation?.resume(with: result)
+    }
+}
+
+struct DraftHeldProcessor: CardPhotoProcessing {
+    let gate: DraftGate<ProcessedCardPhoto>
+    func process(_ data: Data) async throws -> ProcessedCardPhoto { try await gate.run() }
+}
+
+@MainActor
+final class DraftLoadCounter {
+    var calls = 0
+    func input(_ value: Data?) -> CardPhotoInput {
+        CardPhotoInput(load: { self.calls += 1; return value })
+    }
+}
+
+@MainActor
+final class DraftIDs {
+    private var next = 101
+    func make() -> UUID {
+        defer { next += 1 }
+        return UUID(uuidString: String(format: "00000000-0000-0000-0000-%012d", next))!
+    }
+}
