+import Foundation
+import XCTest
+@testable import FocusMate
+
+@MainActor
+final class CardCustomizationControllerTests: XCTestCase {
+    private let a = UUID(uuidString: "00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001")!
+    private let b = UUID(uuidString: "00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000002")!
+    private let c = UUID(uuidString: "00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000003")!
+    private let unknown = UUID(uuidString: "00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000099")!
+    private let blue = CardColor(red: 0.1, green: 0.2, blue: 0.8)
+    // Independent RGB literal, exercised through restore + undo, never a constant-only test.
+    private var defaultStyle: CardStyle {
+        CardStyle(background: CardColor(red: 18.0 / 255, green: 20.0 / 255, blue: 22.0 / 255), photos: [])
+    }
+    private func photo(_ id: UUID, x: Double = 0.5, y: Double = 0.5,
+                       width: Double = 0.6) -> CardPhoto {
+        CardPhotoTestFixtures.photo(id: id, x: x, y: y, fraction: width)
+    }
+    private func style(_ photos: [CardPhoto]) -> CardStyle {
+        CardStyle(background: blue, photos: photos)
+    }
+    private struct Rig {
+        let controller: CardCustomizationController
+        let saver: DraftCardSaver
+    }
+    private func rig(_ initial: CardStyle? = nil,
+                     processor: any CardPhotoProcessing = DraftPhotoProcessor()) -> Rig {
+        let saver = DraftCardSaver()
+        let ids = DraftIDs()
+        let card = CardSnapshot(id: "FM-01234567", issuedAt: Date(timeIntervalSince1970: 1_791_882_000),
+                                style: initial ?? defaultStyle)
+        return Rig(controller: CardCustomizationController(card: card, saver: saver,
+                    processor: processor, makeID: { ids.make() }), saver: saver)
+    }
+    private func input(_ byte: UInt8) -> CardPhotoInput {
+        CardPhotoInput(load: { Data([byte]) })
+    }
+    private func sixPhotos() -> [CardPhoto] {
+        (1...6).map { photo(UUID(uuidString: String(format: "00000000-0000-0000-0000-%012d", $0))!) }
+    }
+
+    // AC 03,04,26; Given: 저장 외형과 고정 ID의 카드; When: 편집 후 명시적 저장; Then: 초안은 저장 외형에서 시작하며 saver는 원래 ID와 편집 외형을 받는다.
+    // 깨짐 명명: 초안을 기본값으로 대체하거나 저장 ID를 새로 생성.
+    func testInitialDraftCopiesSavedStyleAndSaveUsesOriginalCardID() {
+        let initial = style([photo(a)])
+        let r = rig(initial)
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft, initial)
+        r.controller.setBackground(CardColor(red: 0.7, green: 0.3, blue: 0.2))
+        let expected = CardStyle(background: CardColor(red: 0.7, green: 0.3, blue: 0.2), photos: [photo(a)])
+        XCTAssertTrue(r.controller.save())
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.saver.calls.count, 1)
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.saver.calls.first?.cardID, "FM-01234567")
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.saver.calls.first?.style, expected)
+    }
+
+    // AC 03; Given: 기본색 사진0장; When: 사용자색 선택; Then: 초안 색만 바뀌고 저장 호출 없음.
+    // 깨짐 명명: setBackground에서 saver 호출 또는 색 무시.
+    func testBackgroundChoiceChangesDraftWithoutSaving() {
+        let r = rig()
+        r.controller.setBackground(blue)
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft.background, blue)
+        XCTAssertTrue(r.controller.hasChanges)
+        XCTAssertTrue(r.saver.calls.isEmpty)
+    }
+
+    // AC 03; Given: 변경 없는 초안; When: 범위 밖 또는 비유한 RGB 적용; Then: 초안과 history 유지.
+    // 깨짐 명명: RGB 유효성 검사 제거.
+    func testInvalidRGBIsWholeNoOp() {
+        let r = rig()
+        for color in [CardColor(red: -0.1, green: 0, blue: 0), CardColor(red: 1.1, green: 0, blue: 0), CardColor(red: .nan, green: 0, blue: 0)] {
+            r.controller.setBackground(color)
+            XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft, defaultStyle)
+            XCTAssertFalse(r.controller.canUndo)
+        }
+    }
+
+    // AC 04; Given: 사진0장; When: 사진 읽기 성공; Then: 새 사진 중심과 너비가 초기값이며 이동 가능.
+    // 깨짐 명명: 초기 배치가 0 또는 이동 메서드가 no-op.
+    func testImportCreatesEditablePhotoWithLiteralInitialPlacement() async {
+        let r = rig()
+        await r.controller.importPhotos([input(1)])
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft.photos.count, 1)
+        let importedID = UUID(uuidString: "00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000101")!
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft.photos.first?.id, importedID)
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft.photos.first?.centerX, 0.5)
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft.photos.first?.centerY, 0.5)
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft.photos.first?.widthFraction, 0.6)
+        r.controller.selectPhoto(importedID)
+        r.controller.setPlacement(photoID: importedID, centerX: 0.2, centerY: 0.3, widthFraction: 0.8)
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.selectedPhotoID, importedID)
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft.photos.first?.centerX, 0.2)
+        XCTAssertTrue(r.saver.calls.isEmpty)
+    }
+
+    // AC 06; Given: 6장; When: 추가 요청; Then: loader0회 maximumPhotos.
+    // 깨짐 명명: 6장 가드 뒤에 loader 호출.
+    func testFullCardRejectsImportBeforeLoader() async {
+        let r = rig(style(sixPhotos()))
+        let loader = DraftLoadCounter()
+        await r.controller.importPhotos([loader.input(Data([1]))])
+        XCTAssertEqual(loader.calls, 0)
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft.photos.count, 6)
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.notice, .maximumPhotos)
+        XCTAssertFalse(r.controller.canAddPhotos)
+        XCTAssertFalse(r.controller.canUndo)
+    }
+
+    // AC 07; Given: 5장; When: 정상3장 선택; Then: 첫1장만 추가하고 나머지 loader 미호출.
+    // 깨짐 명명: 만석 이후 입력 처리 또는 omittedCount 오계산.
+    func testOverflowProcessesOnlyRemainingSlots() async {
+        let r = rig(style(Array(sixPhotos().prefix(5))))
+        let loader = DraftLoadCounter()
+        await r.controller.importPhotos([loader.input(Data([7])), loader.input(Data([8])), loader.input(Data([9]))])
+        XCTAssertEqual(loader.calls, 1)
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft.photos.count, 6)
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft.photos.last?.data, Data([7]))
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.notice, .partialImport(failures: [], omittedCount: 2))
+    }
+
+    // AC 07,09; Given: 5장; When: 실패 다음 성공 다음 미처리 입력; Then: 성공이 마지막 자리를 쓰고 실패 생략 안내.
+    // 깨짐 명명: 실패를 자리 소비로 계산.
+    func testFailureDoesNotConsumeRemainingSlot() async {
+        let r = rig(style(Array(sixPhotos().prefix(5))))
+        let loader = DraftLoadCounter()
+        await r.controller.importPhotos([loader.input(nil), loader.input(Data([7])), loader.input(Data([8]))])
+        XCTAssertEqual(loader.calls, 2)
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft.photos.count, 6)
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft.photos.last?.data, Data([7]))
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.notice, .partialImport(failures: [.unreadable], omittedCount: 1))
+    }
+
+    // AC 08; Given: 2장 이력 없음; When: 빈 선택; Then: 외형과 history 유지.
+    // 깨짐 명명: 빈 선택이 history를 추가.
+    func testEmptyPickerCancellationPreservesDraftWithoutHistory() async {
+        let initial = style([photo(a), photo(b)])
+        let r = rig(initial)
+        await r.controller.importPhotos([])
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft, initial)
+        XCTAssertFalse(r.controller.canUndo)
+        XCTAssertFalse(r.controller.isImporting)
+    }
+
+    // AC 08,10; Given: 실패 안내 있는 세션; When: 빈 선택; Then: notice 유지.
+    // 깨짐 명명: 빈 배열 분기가 notice 초기화.
+    func testEmptyInputKeepsExistingFailureNotice() async {
+        let r = rig()
+        await r.controller.importPhotos([CardPhotoInput(load: { nil })])
+        await r.controller.importPhotos([])
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.notice, .importFailed([.unreadable]))
+    }
+
+    // AC 09,15; Given: 기존1장; When: 정상 실패 정상 입력; Then: 성공 순서 보존하고 undo1회로 전체 batch 제거.
+    // 깨짐 명명: 성공마다 history를 만들거나 순서를 역전.
+    func testPartialSuccessIsOneUndoBatch() async {
+        let initial = style([photo(a)])
+        let r = rig(initial)
+        await r.controller.importPhotos([input(2), input(90), input(3)])
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft.photos.map(\.data), [Data([1]), Data([2]), Data([3])])
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.notice, .partialImport(failures: [.encodingFailed], omittedCount: 0))
+        r.controller.undo()
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft, initial)
+        XCTAssertFalse(r.controller.canUndo)
+    }
+
+    // AC 09; Given: 부분 성공 안내; When: 색 편집 후 저장; Then: 입력 잠금 해제되고 저장 허용.
+    // 깨짐 명명: 실패 후 isImporting 해제 누락.
+    func testPartialFailureAllowsEditingAndSave() async {
+        let r = rig(style([photo(a)]))
+        await r.controller.importPhotos([input(2), input(90)])
+        XCTAssertFalse(r.controller.isImporting)
+        r.controller.setBackground(CardColor(red: 0.9, green: 0.8, blue: 0.7))
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft.background, CardColor(red: 0.9, green: 0.8, blue: 0.7))
+        XCTAssertTrue(r.controller.save())
+    }
+
+    // AC 10; Given: 색 변경 이력; When: processor실패 둘; Then: 외형 이력 유지 실패 선택순 안내.
+    // 깨짐 명명: 실패 batch가 history를 지우거나 오류 순서 역전.
+    func testAllFailuresKeepPreviousHistoryAndFailureOrder() async {
+        let r = rig()
+        r.controller.setBackground(blue)
+        let edited = r.controller.draft
+        await r.controller.importPhotos([input(91), input(90)])
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft, edited)
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.notice, .importFailed([.unsupportedAspectRatio, .encodingFailed]))
+        r.controller.undo()
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft, defaultStyle)
+        XCTAssertFalse(r.controller.canUndo)
+    }
+
+    // AC 10; Given: 활성 세션; When: nil 일반오류 cancellation 입력; Then: 세 실패 unreadable 후 재입력 가능.
+    // 깨짐 명명: loader 오류를 삼키거나 cancellation을 세션 폐기로 처리.
+    func testLoaderNilThrowAndActiveCancellationAreUnreadable() async {
+        let r = rig()
+        await r.controller.importPhotos([
+            CardPhotoInput(load: { nil }),
+            CardPhotoInput(load: { throw CardPhotoProcessingIssue.encodingFailed }),
+            CardPhotoInput(load: { throw CancellationError() })
+        ])
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.notice, .importFailed([.unreadable, .unreadable, .unreadable]))
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft, defaultStyle)
+        XCTAssertFalse(r.controller.canUndo)
+        await r.controller.importPhotos([input(1)])
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft.photos.count, 1)
+        XCTAssertNil(r.controller.notice)
+    }
+
+    // AC 11; Given: 서로 다른 배치의2장; When: 한장 배치 변경; Then: 선택 대상 배치만 변경 치수와 나머지 보존.
+    // 깨짐 명명: 모든 사진에 배치 적용 또는 치수 변경.
+    func testPlacementChangesOnlyTargetAndKeepsAspect() {
+        let initial = style([photo(a), photo(b, x: 0.9, y: 0.1, width: 0.3)])
+        let r = rig(initial)
+        r.controller.selectPhoto(a)
+        r.controller.setPlacement(photoID: a, centerX: 0.2, centerY: 0.7, widthFraction: 1.2)
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft.photos[0], photo(a, x: 0.2, y: 0.7, width: 1.2))
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft.photos[1], photo(b, x: 0.9, y: 0.1, width: 0.3))
+        XCTAssertTrue(r.saver.calls.isEmpty)
+    }
+
+    // AC 11; Given: 한장; When: 범위 밖 배치; Then: 각축 너비 clamp.
+    // 깨짐 명명: clamp 하한 또는 상한 오설정.
+    func testPlacementClampsMinimum() {
+        let r = rig(style([photo(a)]))
+        r.controller.setPlacement(photoID: a, centerX: -2, centerY: -3, widthFraction: 0.01)
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft.photos[0], photo(a, x: -1, y: -1, width: 0.15))
+        r.controller.undo()
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft.photos[0], photo(a))
+    }
+
+    // AC 11; Given: 한장; When: 범위 밖 배치; Then: 각축 너비 clamp.
+    // 깨짐 명명: clamp 하한 또는 상한 오설정.
+    func testPlacementClampsMaximum() {
+        let r = rig(style([photo(a)]))
+        r.controller.setPlacement(photoID: a, centerX: 3, centerY: 4, widthFraction: 3)
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft.photos[0], photo(a, x: 2, y: 2, width: 2.5))
+        r.controller.undo()
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft.photos[0], photo(a))
+    }
+
+    // AC 11; Given: 정상 배치; When: 한 필드만 비유한 다른 필드는 정상 변경; Then: 전체 no-op history없음.
+    // 깨짐 명명: finite 필드만 부분 적용.
+    func testNonFinitePlacementRejectsWholeOperation() {
+        let initial = style([photo(a)])
+        let r = rig(initial)
+        for values in [(Double.nan, 0.2, 0.9), (0.2, Double.infinity, 0.9), (0.2, 0.3, -Double.infinity)] {
+            r.controller.setPlacement(photoID: a, centerX: values.0, centerY: values.1, widthFraction: values.2)
+            XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft, initial)
+            XCTAssertFalse(r.controller.canUndo)
+        }
+    }
+
+    // AC 13; Given: A B C; When: B를 한단계 이동; Then: 순서만 변경.
+    // 깨짐 명명: 앞뒤 방향 반전 또는 끝으로 이동.
+    func testLayerMovesOneStepForward() {
+        let r = rig(style([photo(a), photo(b), photo(c)]))
+        r.controller.selectPhoto(b)
+        r.controller.moveLayer(photoID: b, direction: .forward)
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft.photos.map(\.id), [a, c, b])
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft.photos.first(where: { $0.id == b }), photo(b))
+    }
+
+    // AC 13; Given: A B C; When: B를 한단계 이동; Then: 순서만 변경.
+    // 깨짐 명명: 앞뒤 방향 반전 또는 끝으로 이동.
+    func testLayerMovesOneStepBackward() {
+        let r = rig(style([photo(a), photo(b), photo(c)]))
+        r.controller.selectPhoto(b)
+        r.controller.moveLayer(photoID: b, direction: .backward)
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft.photos.map(\.id), [b, a, c])
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft.photos.first(where: { $0.id == b }), photo(b))
+    }
+
+    // AC 13,16; Given: A B C; When: 맨앞 forward 맨뒤 backward; Then: 외형 이력 유지.
+    // 깨짐 명명: 끝단 이동이 history 생성.
+    func testLayerEndsDoNotCreateHistory() {
+        let initial = style([photo(a), photo(b), photo(c)])
+        let r = rig(initial)
+        r.controller.moveLayer(photoID: c, direction: .forward)
+        r.controller.moveLayer(photoID: a, direction: .backward)
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft, initial)
+        XCTAssertFalse(r.controller.canUndo)
+    }
+
+    // AC 14,15; Given: 6장; When: 선택 사진 삭제 후 undo; Then: 해당사진만 제거 슬롯 재개 undo복원.
+    // 깨짐 명명: 다른 사진 삭제 또는 undo에서 원본 배치 손실.
+    func testDeleteReopensSlotAndUndoRestoresExactPhoto() {
+        let initial = style(sixPhotos())
+        let r = rig(initial)
+        r.controller.selectPhoto(b)
+        r.controller.deletePhoto(b)
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft.photos.map(\.id), [a, c,
+            UUID(uuidString: "00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000004")!,
+            UUID(uuidString: "00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000005")!,
+            UUID(uuidString: "00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000006")!])
+        XCTAssertTrue(r.controller.canAddPhotos)
+        r.controller.undo()
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft, initial)
+    }
+
+    // AC 15; Given: 기존1장; When: 추가 색 이동 크기 순서 삭제를 수행 후 역 undo; Then: 매번 직전 외형 복원 저장0회.
+    // 깨짐 명명: 한 변이 history 누락.
+    func testUndoReversesEachMutationWithoutSaving() async {
+        let initial = style([photo(a)])
+        let r = rig(initial)
+        await r.controller.importPhotos([input(2)])
+        let added = r.controller.draft
+        r.controller.setBackground(CardColor(red: 0.9, green: 0.8, blue: 0.7))
+        let colored = r.controller.draft
+        r.controller.setPlacement(photoID: a, centerX: 0.2, centerY: 0.3, widthFraction: 0.6)
+        let moved = r.controller.draft
+        r.controller.setPlacement(photoID: a, centerX: 0.2, centerY: 0.3, widthFraction: 1.2)
+        let sized = r.controller.draft
+        r.controller.moveLayer(photoID: a, direction: .forward)
+        let layered = r.controller.draft
+        r.controller.deletePhoto(a)
+        for expected in [layered, sized, moved, colored, added, initial] {
+            r.controller.undo()
+            XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft, expected)
+        }
+        XCTAssertFalse(r.controller.canUndo)
+        XCTAssertTrue(r.saver.calls.isEmpty)
+    }
+
+    // AC 15; Given: 추가 사진 선택; When: 추가 undo; Then: 선택nil.
+    // 깨짐 명명: undo후 존재하지 않는 선택 유지.
+    func testUndoRemovedSelectedPhotoClearsSelection() async {
+        let r = rig()
+        await r.controller.importPhotos([input(1)])
+        r.controller.selectPhoto(UUID(uuidString: "00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000101")!)
+        r.controller.undo()
+        XCTAssertNil(r.controller.selectedPhotoID)
+    }
+
+    // AC 16; Given: 서로 다른 색 변경21회; When: undo20회; Then: 첫 변경 0.01 남고 더 undo불가.
+    // 깨짐 명명: history 21개 유지 또는 19개만 유지.
+    func testUndoRetainsLiteralFirstChangeAfterTwentyUndos() {
+        let r = rig()
+        for step in 1...21 {
+            r.controller.setBackground(CardColor(red: Double(step) / 100, green: 0.3, blue: 0.4))
+        }
+        for _ in 0..<20 { r.controller.undo() }
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft.background, CardColor(red: 0.01, green: 0.3, blue: 0.4))
+        XCTAssertFalse(r.controller.canUndo)
+        r.controller.undo()
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft.background, CardColor(red: 0.01, green: 0.3, blue: 0.4))
+    }
+
+    // AC 16; Given: 기존1장과 한 색 변경; When: 선택 해제 재선택 같은색 같은배치; Then: undo1회로 baseline.
+    // 깨짐 명명: 선택이나 동일값에 history 생성.
+    func testSelectionAndEqualValuesDoNotConsumeHistory() {
+        let initial = style([photo(a)])
+        let r = rig(initial)
+        let chosen = CardColor(red: 0.6, green: 0.7, blue: 0.8)
+        r.controller.setBackground(chosen)
+        r.controller.selectPhoto(a)
+        r.controller.selectPhoto(nil)
+        r.controller.selectPhoto(a)
+        r.controller.setBackground(chosen)
+        r.controller.setPlacement(photoID: a, centerX: 0.5, centerY: 0.5, widthFraction: 0.6)
+        r.controller.undo()
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft, initial)
+        XCTAssertFalse(r.controller.canUndo)
+    }
+
+    // AC 11,13,14,16; Given: 알려진 사진 선택; When: 없는ID 선택 변경 삭제 순서; Then: 선택 초안 이력 유지.
+    // 깨짐 명명: unknownID 선택 허용 또는 변경 생성.
+    func testUnknownIDsAreNoOpsIncludingSelection() {
+        let initial = style([photo(a)])
+        let r = rig(initial)
+        r.controller.selectPhoto(a)
+        r.controller.selectPhoto(unknown)
+        r.controller.setPlacement(photoID: unknown, centerX: 1, centerY: 1, widthFraction: 1)
+        r.controller.moveLayer(photoID: unknown, direction: .forward)
+        r.controller.deletePhoto(unknown)
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.selectedPhotoID, a)
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft, initial)
+        XCTAssertFalse(r.controller.canUndo)
+    }
+
+    // AC 17; Given: 다른색 사진3장; When: 기본복원 후 undo; Then: literal기본색0장 복원 전 외형 undo.
+    // 깨짐 명명: 복원이 사진만 지우거나 history 누락.
+    func testRestoreDefaultCanBeUndoneAsOneMutation() {
+        let initial = style([photo(a), photo(b), photo(c)])
+        let r = rig(initial)
+        r.controller.restoreDefault()
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft, defaultStyle)
+        XCTAssertTrue(r.saver.calls.isEmpty)
+        r.controller.undo()
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft, initial)
+        XCTAssertFalse(r.controller.canUndo)
+    }
+
+    // AC 16,17; Given: 기본 초안; When: 기본복원; Then: history없음.
+    // 깨짐 명명: 동일기본 복원이 history 생성.
+    func testRestoringAlreadyDefaultIsNoOp() {
+        let r = rig()
+        r.controller.restoreDefault()
+        XCTAssertFalse(r.controller.canUndo)
+        XCTAssertFalse(r.controller.hasChanges)
+    }

+    // AC 19; Given: 변경없음; When: 종료; Then: dismiss 저장0회.

+    func testPristineExitDismissesWithoutSaving() {

+    // AC 20; Given: 미저장색 변경; When: 종료확인 계속편집; Then: 초안과 history 유지.

+    func testContinueEditingPreservesUnsavedDraftAndHistory() {

+    // AC 21; Given: 미저장변경 선택 실패안내; When: 버리기; Then: baseline와 비활성 초기상태.
+    // 깨짐 명명: discard가 draft selection notice history 잔존.
+    func testDiscardRestoresBaselineAndClearsSessionState() async {
+        let initial = style([photo(a)])
+        let r = rig(initial)
+        r.controller.setBackground(CardColor(red: 0.9, green: 0.8, blue: 0.7))
+        r.controller.selectPhoto(a)
+        await r.controller.importPhotos([input(90)])
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.requestExit(), .confirmDiscard)
+        r.controller.discard()
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft, initial)
+        XCTAssertNil(r.controller.selectedPhotoID)
+        XCTAssertNil(r.controller.notice)
+        XCTAssertFalse(r.controller.canUndo)
+        XCTAssertFalse(r.controller.hasChanges)
+        XCTAssertFalse(r.controller.isImporting)
+        XCTAssertFalse(r.controller.isSaving)
+        XCTAssertFalse(r.controller.didSave)
+        XCTAssertTrue(r.saver.calls.isEmpty)
+    }
+
+    // AC 21,22; Given: 버린 세션; When: 모든변경 import save 호출; Then: draft selection notice유지 savefalse 경계0회.
+    // 깨짐 명명: 한 메서드의 비활성 가드 누락.
+    func testInactiveSessionRejectsAllDefinedMutationMethods() async {
+        let initial = style([photo(a), photo(b)])
+        let r = rig(initial)
+        func assertInactive(file: StaticString = #filePath, line: UInt = #line) {
+            XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft, initial, file: file, line: line)
+            XCTAssertNil(r.controller.selectedPhotoID, file: file, line: line)
+            XCTAssertNil(r.controller.notice, file: file, line: line)
+            XCTAssertFalse(r.controller.canUndo, file: file, line: line)
+            XCTAssertFalse(r.controller.didSave, file: file, line: line)
+            XCTAssertFalse(r.controller.isImporting, file: file, line: line)
+            XCTAssertFalse(r.controller.isSaving, file: file, line: line)
+            XCTAssertTrue(r.saver.calls.isEmpty, file: file, line: line)
+        }
+        r.controller.discard()
+        assertInactive()
+        r.controller.setBackground(CardColor(red: 0.9, green: 0.8, blue: 0.7))
+        assertInactive()
+        r.controller.selectPhoto(a)
+        assertInactive()
+        r.controller.selectPhoto(nil)
+        assertInactive()
+        r.controller.setPlacement(photoID: a, centerX: 1, centerY: 1, widthFraction: 1)
+        assertInactive()
+        r.controller.moveLayer(photoID: a, direction: .forward)
+        assertInactive()
+        r.controller.deletePhoto(a)
+        assertInactive()
+        r.controller.restoreDefault()
+        assertInactive()
+        r.controller.undo()
+        assertInactive()
+        r.controller.continueEditing()
+        assertInactive()
+        r.controller.discard()
+        let loader = DraftLoadCounter()
+        await r.controller.importPhotos([loader.input(Data([2]))])
+        XCTAssertFalse(r.controller.save())
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft, initial)
+        XCTAssertNil(r.controller.selectedPhotoID)
+        XCTAssertNil(r.controller.notice)
+        XCTAssertFalse(r.controller.canUndo)
+        XCTAssertFalse(r.controller.didSave)
+        XCTAssertFalse(r.controller.isImporting)
+        XCTAssertEqual(loader.calls, 0)
+        XCTAssertTrue(r.saver.calls.isEmpty)
+    }
+
+    // AC 19,21; Given: 버려 비활성인 세션; When: 종료를 다시 요청; Then: 상태 변경 없이 dismiss.
+    // 깨짐 명명: 비활성 requestExit가 confirmDiscard를 반환하거나 세션 상태를 다시 활성화.
+    func testInactiveRequestExitDismissesWithoutStateChanges() {
+        let initial = style([photo(a), photo(b)])
+        let r = rig(initial)

+

+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft, initial)
+        XCTAssertNil(r.controller.selectedPhotoID)
+        XCTAssertNil(r.controller.notice)
+        XCTAssertFalse(r.controller.canUndo)
+        XCTAssertFalse(r.controller.hasChanges)
+        XCTAssertFalse(r.controller.didSave)
+        XCTAssertFalse(r.controller.isImporting)
+        XCTAssertFalse(r.controller.isSaving)

+    }

+    // AC 23; Given: loader 대기 중 기존 2장과 색 변경 이력; When: 변경재입력저장선택종료 요청; Then: 변경잠금 선택 종료확인 허용.
+    // 깨짐 명명: import 잠금 가드 누락 또는 선택/종료까지 차단.
+    func testImportBlocksMutationsReimportAndSaveButAllowsSelectionAndExit() async {
+        let initial = style([photo(a), photo(b)])
+        let r = rig(initial)
+        r.controller.setBackground(CardColor(red: 0.9, green: 0.8, blue: 0.7))
+        let expected = CardStyle(background: CardColor(red: 0.9, green: 0.8, blue: 0.7), photos: [photo(a), photo(b)])
+        let gate = DraftGate<Data?>()
+        let task = Task { await r.controller.importPhotos([CardPhotoInput(load: { try await gate.run() })]) }
+        await gate.waitForStart()
+        XCTAssertTrue(r.controller.isImporting)
+        r.controller.setBackground(CardColor(red: 0.2, green: 0.4, blue: 0.6))
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft, expected)
+        r.controller.setPlacement(photoID: a, centerX: 1, centerY: 1, widthFraction: 1)
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft, expected)
+        r.controller.moveLayer(photoID: a, direction: .forward)
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft, expected)
+        r.controller.deletePhoto(a)
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft, expected)
+        r.controller.undo()
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft, expected)
+        r.controller.restoreDefault()
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft, expected)
+        let loader = DraftLoadCounter()
+        await r.controller.importPhotos([loader.input(Data([3]))])
+        XCTAssertFalse(r.controller.save())
+        r.controller.selectPhoto(b)
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.selectedPhotoID, b)
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.requestExit(), .confirmDiscard)
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft, expected)
+        XCTAssertTrue(r.controller.canUndo)
+        XCTAssertEqual(loader.calls, 0)
+        XCTAssertTrue(r.saver.calls.isEmpty)
+        await gate.release(.success(Data([2])))
+        await task.value
+        XCTAssertFalse(r.controller.isImporting)
+        r.controller.setBackground(CardColor(red: 0.2, green: 0.4, blue: 0.6))
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft.background, CardColor(red: 0.2, green: 0.4, blue: 0.6))
+        XCTAssertTrue(r.controller.save())
+    }

+    // AC 26; Given: 변경 draft와 선택; When: saverfalse; Then: 명시적오류 또는 fallback 안내 draft 이력 선택 유지.
+    // 깨짐 명명: 실패를 성공처리 또는 실패에서 draft/history 초기화.
+    func testSaveFailureFallbackKeepsDraftSelectionAndHistory() {
+        let initial = style([photo(a)])
+        let r = rig(initial)
+        r.controller.setBackground(CardColor(red: 0.9, green: 0.8, blue: 0.7))
+        r.controller.selectPhoto(a)
+        let edited = r.controller.draft
+        r.saver.result = false
+        r.saver.saveFailure = nil
+        XCTAssertFalse(r.controller.save())
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.notice, .saveFailed(.writeFailed))
+        XCTAssertFalse(r.controller.didSave)
+        XCTAssertFalse(r.controller.isSaving)

+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.selectedPhotoID, a)
+        XCTAssertTrue(r.controller.canUndo)
+        r.controller.undo()
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft, initial)
+    }

+    // AC 26; Given: 변경 draft와 선택; When: saverfalse; Then: 명시적오류 또는 fallback 안내 draft 이력 선택 유지.
+    // 깨짐 명명: 실패를 성공처리 또는 실패에서 draft/history 초기화.
+    func testSaveFailureExplicitKeepsDraftSelectionAndHistory() {
+        let initial = style([photo(a)])
+        let r = rig(initial)
+        r.controller.setBackground(CardColor(red: 0.9, green: 0.8, blue: 0.7))
+        r.controller.selectPhoto(a)
+        let edited = r.controller.draft
+        r.saver.result = false
+        r.saver.saveFailure = .notReady
+        XCTAssertFalse(r.controller.save())
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.notice, .saveFailed(.notReady))
+        XCTAssertFalse(r.controller.didSave)
+        XCTAssertFalse(r.controller.isSaving)
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft, edited)
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.selectedPhotoID, a)
+        XCTAssertTrue(r.controller.canUndo)
+        r.controller.undo()
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft, initial)
+    }
+
+    // AC 23,26; Given: 변경 초안과 동기 saver callback; When: 저장 callback 안에서 다시 저장; Then: 저장잠금이 보이고 중첩저장 차단, 외부저장만 성공.
+    // 깨짐 명명: isSaving을 saver 호출 뒤 설정하거나 callback 재진입 save를 허용해 saver를 두 번 호출.
+    func testSynchronousSaverCallbackObservesSavingLockAndBlocksReentrantSave() {
+        let r = rig()
+        let editedColor = CardColor(red: 0.9, green: 0.8, blue: 0.7)
+        let expected = CardStyle(background: editedColor, photos: [])
+        r.controller.setBackground(editedColor)
+        var callbackCount = 0
+        var observedIsSaving: Bool?
+        var nestedSaveResult: Bool?
+        var callsDuringCallback: Int?
+        r.saver.onSave = { [weak controller = r.controller, weak saver = r.saver] in
+            guard let controller, let saver else { return }
+            callbackCount += 1
+            observedIsSaving = controller.isSaving
+            saver.onSave = nil
+            nestedSaveResult = controller.save()
+            callsDuringCallback = saver.calls.count
+        }
+
+        XCTAssertTrue(r.controller.save())
+        r.saver.onSave = nil
+
+        XCTAssertEqual(callbackCount, 1)
+        XCTAssertEqual(observedIsSaving, true)
+        XCTAssertEqual(nestedSaveResult, false)
+        XCTAssertEqual(callsDuringCallback, 1)
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.saver.calls.count, 1)
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.saver.calls.first?.cardID, "FM-01234567")
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.saver.calls.first?.style, expected)
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft, expected)
+        XCTAssertTrue(r.controller.didSave)
+        XCTAssertFalse(r.controller.isSaving)
+        XCTAssertFalse(r.controller.hasChanges)
+        XCTAssertFalse(r.controller.canUndo)
+        XCTAssertNil(r.controller.notice)
+    }
+
+    // AC 26; Given: 저장 실패한 변경; When: 재시도 성공 후 새 변경과 버리기; Then: 성공 안내상태 historyclear 새 baseline 유지.
+    // 깨짐 명명: 성공 baseline갱신 또는 history/notice 초기화 누락.
+    func testRetrySuccessUpdatesBaselineClearsHistoryAndNotice() {
+        let r = rig()
+        r.controller.setBackground(blue)
+        r.saver.result = false
+        XCTAssertFalse(r.controller.save())
+        r.saver.result = true
+        XCTAssertTrue(r.controller.save())
+        XCTAssertTrue(r.controller.didSave)
+        XCTAssertNil(r.controller.notice)
+        XCTAssertFalse(r.controller.canUndo)
+        XCTAssertFalse(r.controller.hasChanges)
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.requestExit(), .dismiss)
+        r.controller.setBackground(CardColor(red: 0.9, green: 0.8, blue: 0.7))
+        r.controller.discard()
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft.background, blue)
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.saver.calls.count, 2)
+    }
+
+    // AC 04,16; Given: 기존 사진 선택; When: select(nil); Then: 선택 해제만 발생하고 초안/history 유지.
+    // 깨짐 명명: nil 선택을 무시하거나 선택 해제에 history 생성.
+    func testDeselectClearsOnlySelection() {
+        let initial = style([photo(a)])
+        let r = rig(initial)
+        r.controller.selectPhoto(a)
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.selectedPhotoID, a)
+        r.controller.selectPhoto(nil)
+        XCTAssertNil(r.controller.selectedPhotoID)
+        XCTAssertEqual(r.controller.draft, initial)
+        XCTAssertFalse(r.controller.canUndo)
+        XCTAssertTrue(r.saver.calls.isEmpty)
+    }
+}

+import Foundation
+import XCTest
+@testable import FocusMate
+
+final class CardCustomizationNoticeMessageTests: XCTestCase {
+    func testOversizedInputExplainsTheEightMiBInputLimit() {
+        // Regression: a typed oversized input is reduced to a generic import failure.
+        let message = CardCustomizationNotice.importFailed([.inputTooLarge]).message
+        assertLimit("8MiB", in: message)
+        assertContainsAny(["입력", "원본", "선택한 사진"], in: message)
+    }
+
+    func testUnsupportedAspectRatioExplainsBothAllowedBounds() {
+        let message = CardCustomizationNotice.importFailed([.unsupportedAspectRatio]).message
+        XCTAssertTrue(message.contains("비율"))
+        XCTAssertTrue(message.contains("0.1"))
+        XCTAssertTrue(message.contains("10"))
+    }
+
+    func testCompressionLimitAndEncodingFailureExplainTheirDistinctCauses() {
+        let limitFailure = CardCustomizationNotice.importFailed([.cannotMeetStorageLimit]).message
+        assertLimit("1MiB", in: limitFailure)
+        assertContainsAny(["변환", "압축", "저장"], in: limitFailure)
+
+        let encodingFailure = CardCustomizationNotice.importFailed([.encodingFailed]).message
+        assertContainsAny(["변환", "인코딩"], in: encodingFailure)
+        assertContainsAny(["실패", "못", "오류"], in: encodingFailure)
+        assertContainsAny(["다시", "재선택", "선택"], in: encodingFailure)
+        let compact = compactWhitespace(encodingFailure)
+        XCTAssertFalse(compact.contains("1MiB초과") || compact.contains("1MiB를초과"),
+                       "An encoding failure alone must not claim that the photo exceeded 1MiB")
+    }
+
+    func testOversizedStoredPhotoExplainsThePerPhotoOneMiBLimit() {
+        let id = UUID(uuidString: "00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001")!
+        let message = CardCustomizationNotice.saveFailed(
+            .invalidState(.invalidCardStyle(.photoTooLarge(id)))
+        ).message
+        assertLimit("1MiB", in: message)
+        assertContainsAny(["사진 한 장", "사진당", "장당", "각 사진"], in: message)
+    }
+
+    func testOversizedPhotoTotalExplainsSixMiBAndPreservedDraftRetry() {
+        let message = CardCustomizationNotice.saveFailed(
+            .invalidState(.invalidCardStyle(.totalPhotosTooLarge))
+        ).message
+        assertLimit("6MiB", in: message)
+        assertContainsAny(["전체", "합계", "합산", "모든 사진"], in: message)
+        assertDraftPreservedAndRetry(message)
+    }
+
+    func testPartialImportKeepsDistinctFailureReasonsAndSixPhotoOmissionReason() {
+        let message = CardCustomizationNotice.partialImport(
+            failures: [.inputTooLarge, .unsupportedAspectRatio, .cannotMeetStorageLimit],
+            omittedCount: 2
+        ).message
+        assertLimit("8MiB", in: message)
+        XCTAssertTrue(message.contains("비율"))
+        XCTAssertTrue(message.contains("0.1"))
+        XCTAssertTrue(message.contains("10"))
+        assertLimit("1MiB", in: message)
+        let compact = compactWhitespace(message)
+        XCTAssertTrue(compact.contains("6장"), "Partial import must retain the six-photo capacity reason")
+        assertContainsAny(["제외", "누락", "추가하지", "추가되지", "초과"], in: message)
+    }
+
+    func testRepeatedFailuresDoNotRepeatTheSameLimitExplanation() {
+        let distinct = CardCustomizationNotice.partialImport(
+            failures: [.inputTooLarge, .unsupportedAspectRatio], omittedCount: 1
+        ).message
+        let repeated = CardCustomizationNotice.partialImport(
+            failures: [.inputTooLarge, .inputTooLarge, .unsupportedAspectRatio,
+                       .inputTooLarge, .unsupportedAspectRatio], omittedCount: 1
+        ).message
+        assertLimit("8MiB", in: distinct)
+        assertLimit("8MiB", in: repeated)
+        XCTAssertTrue(distinct.contains("0.1"))
+        XCTAssertTrue(repeated.contains("0.1"))
+        XCTAssertEqual(occurrences(of: "8MiB", in: repeated), occurrences(of: "8MiB", in: distinct))
+        XCTAssertEqual(occurrences(of: "0.1", in: repeated), occurrences(of: "0.1", in: distinct))
+    }
+
+    func testUnreadableImportAndSaveFailuresKeepTheirExistingRecoveryGuidance() {
+        let unreadableImport = CardCustomizationNotice.importFailed([.unreadable]).message
+        XCTAssertTrue(unreadableImport.contains("읽"), "Unreadable imports must explain the reading failure")
+        assertContainsAny(["다시", "재선택", "선택"], in: unreadableImport)
+
+        let saveMessages = [
+            CardCustomizationNotice.saveFailed(.unreadable).message,
+            CardCustomizationNotice.saveFailed(.writeFailed).message
+        ]
+        for message in saveMessages {
+            XCTAssertTrue(message.contains("저장"))
+            assertDraftPreservedAndRetry(message)
+        }
+    }
+
+    private func assertLimit(_ limit: String, in message: String,
+                             file: StaticString = #filePath, line: UInt = #line) {
+        XCTAssertTrue(compactWhitespace(message).contains(limit), "Missing specified limit: " + limit,
+                      file: file, line: line)
+        XCTAssertFalse(message.contains("MB"), "Binary limits must use the unambiguous MiB unit",
+                       file: file, line: line)
+    }
+
+    private func assertDraftPreservedAndRetry(_ message: String,
+                                             file: StaticString = #filePath, line: UInt = #line) {
+        XCTAssertTrue(message.contains("초안"), file: file, line: line)
+        assertContainsAny(["유지", "보존", "남아"], in: message, file: file, line: line)
+        assertContainsAny(["다시", "재시도"], in: message, file: file, line: line)
+    }
+
+    private func assertContainsAny(_ words: [String], in message: String,
+                                   file: StaticString = #filePath, line: UInt = #line) {
+        XCTAssertTrue(words.contains { message.contains($0) },
+                      "Missing user-facing explanation from category: " + words.joined(separator: ", "),
+                      file: file, line: line)
+    }
+
+    private func compactWhitespace(_ message: String) -> String {
+        message.filter { !$0.isWhitespace }
+    }
+
+    private func occurrences(of token: String, in message: String) -> Int {
+        compactWhitespace(message).components(separatedBy: token).count - 1
+    }
+}

+import Foundation
+import XCTest
+@testable import FocusMate
+
+final class CardPhotoIntegrityTests: XCTestCase {
+    // 깨짐 명명: PNG chunk CRC와 복구된 thumbnail만 확인하고 잘린 zlib 본문을 허용하면 실패한다.
+    func testPNGRecomputedCRCTruncatedIDATIsUnreadable() async throws {
+        let full = IntegrityPNGFixture.zlibStored(IntegrityPNGFixture.rows(count: 8))
+        // Keep the zlib header and LEN=264/NLEN, but only four of eight scanlines.
+        // IEND is present; all chunk lengths and CRCs describe the new payload.
+        var damaged = Data(full.prefix(7 + 4 * 33))
+        damaged.append(contentsOf: full.suffix(4)) // original Adler-32, not repaired
+        let input = IntegrityPNGFixture.png(idats: [damaged])
+        await assertUnreadable(input)
+    }
+
+    // 깨짐 명명: zlib/Adler-32 검증 후 IHDR 높이에 필요한 래스터 길이를 검사하지 않으면 실패한다.
+    func testPNGValidZlibShortRasterIsUnreadable() async throws {
+        // Valid complete zlib stream and checksum, but one row for IHDR height=8.
+        let short = IntegrityPNGFixture.zlibStored(IntegrityPNGFixture.rows(count: 1))
+        let input = IntegrityPNGFixture.png(idats: [short])
+        await assertUnreadable(input)
+    }
+
+    // 깨짐 명명: IDAT 하나만 읽거나 각 IDAT를 별도 zlib로 처리하면 정상 입력이 거절된다.
+    func testPNGMultipleIDATPreservesColorAndAlpha() async throws {
+        let stream = IntegrityPNGFixture.zlibStored(IntegrityPNGFixture.rows(count: 8))
+        // Split inside the zlib header and inside the raster, not just at block boundaries.
+        let input = IntegrityPNGFixture.png(idats: [
+            Data(stream.prefix(1)), Data(stream.dropFirst(1).prefix(9)),
+            Data(stream.dropFirst(10))
+        ])
+        try await assertAcceptedRGBA(input)
+    }
+
+    // 깨짐 명명: Adam7을 거절하거나 비인터레이스 행 크기로 검증하면 정상 입력이 거절된다.
+    func testPNGAdam7PreservesColorAndAlpha() async throws {
+        let stream = IntegrityPNGFixture.zlibStored(IntegrityPNGFixture.adam7Rows())
+        let input = IntegrityPNGFixture.png(idats: [stream], interlace: 1)
+        try await assertAcceptedRGBA(input)
+    }
+
+    // 깨짐 명명: WebP의 RIFF/VP8L 헤더만 확인하고 불완전 transform 본문을 허용하면 실패한다.
+    func testWebPValidHeaderCorruptBodyIsUnreadable() async throws {
+        let valid = try CardPhotoTestFixtures.losslessWebP()
+        let decoded = try CardPhotoTestFixtures.completeDecoded(valid)
+        XCTAssertEqual(decoded.width, 40, "Independent valid-control fixture")
+        XCTAssertEqual(decoded.height, 20)
+        let input = try corruptedWebP(valid)
+        await assertUnreadable(input)
+    }
+
+    private func assertUnreadable(_ input: Data,
+                                  file: StaticString = #filePath, line: UInt = #line) async {
+        do {
+            _ = try await ImageIOCardPhotoProcessor().process(input)
+            XCTFail("Partially recovered/corrupt input must throw unreadable", file: file, line: line)
+        } catch {
+            XCTAssertEqual(error as? CardPhotoProcessingIssue, .unreadable, file: file, line: line)
+        }
+    }
+
+    private func assertAcceptedRGBA(_ input: Data,
+                                    file: StaticString = #filePath, line: UInt = #line) async throws {
+        // Sanity is SDK decode/draw, never the processor used to generate expected values.
+        let original = try CardPhotoTestFixtures.completeDecoded(input)
+        XCTAssertEqual(original.width, 8, file: file, line: line)
+        XCTAssertEqual(original.height, 8, file: file, line: line)
+        try assertLiteralRGBA(input, file: file, line: line)
+        let result = try await ImageIOCardPhotoProcessor().process(input)
+        let output = try CardPhotoTestFixtures.completeDecoded(result.data)
+        XCTAssertEqual(result.pixelWidth, 8, file: file, line: line)
+        XCTAssertEqual(result.pixelHeight, 8, file: file, line: line)
+        XCTAssertEqual(output.width, 8, file: file, line: line)
+        XCTAssertEqual(output.height, 8, file: file, line: line)
+        try assertLiteralRGBA(result.data, file: file, line: line)
+    }
+
+    private func assertLiteralRGBA(_ bytes: Data,
+                                   file: StaticString, line: UInt) throws {
+        let rgba = try CardPhotoTestFixtures.rgba(bytes)
+        // Hand-derived premultiplied sRGB: (200,80,40) * 128/255 ≈ (100,40,20).
+        // One-unit tolerance permits SDK rounding; alpha=255 or missing pixels fail.
+        XCTAssertEqual(Double(rgba[0]), 100, accuracy: 1, file: file, line: line)
+        XCTAssertEqual(Double(rgba[1]), 40, accuracy: 1, file: file, line: line)
+        XCTAssertEqual(Double(rgba[2]), 20, accuracy: 1, file: file, line: line)
+        XCTAssertEqual(Double(rgba[3]), 128, accuracy: 1, file: file, line: line)
+    }
+
+    private func corruptedWebP(_ valid: Data) throws -> Data {
+        // Single VP8L chunk: RIFF(12) + chunk header(8) + image header(5).
+        guard valid.count >= 25,
+              Data(valid.prefix(4)) == Data("RIFF".utf8),
+              Data(valid[8..<16]) == Data("WEBPVP8L".utf8), valid[20] == 0x2f else {
+            throw DraftFixtureIssue.unexpectedWebPLayout
+        }
+        var payload = Data(valid[20..<25]) // retain actual signature/dimensions/alpha/version
+        // LSB-first: 1=transform present, 11=color indexing; only 5 bits remain,
+        // but its color_table_size requires 8 bits. No palette/pixel body follows.
+        payload.append(0xff)
+        var input = Data("RIFF".utf8)
+        input.append(IntegrityPNGFixture.littleEndian32(18)) // file length 26 minus 8
+        input.append(Data("WEBPVP8L".utf8))
+        input.append(IntegrityPNGFixture.littleEndian32(6)) // even payload: no pad
+        input.append(payload)
+        return input
+    }
+
+    private enum DraftFixtureIssue: Error { case unexpectedWebPLayout }
+}
+
+// Test-only byte builder. No production encoder, ImageIO writer, Pillow, or dependency.
+// W3C PNG: https://www.w3.org/TR/png-3/#8Interlace
+// zlib/DEFLATE: RFC1950 + RFC1951 (one final uncompressed block).
+private enum IntegrityPNGFixture {
+    private static let pixel: [UInt8] = [200, 80, 40, 128] // straight-alpha sRGB
+
+    static func rows(count: Int) -> Data {
+        var raw = Data()
+        for _ in 0..<count {
+            raw.append(0) // filter None
+            for _ in 0..<8 { raw.append(contentsOf: pixel) }
+        }
+        return raw
+    }
+
+    static func adam7Rows() -> Data {
+        // (x origin, y origin, x step, y step), all seven passes populated at 8×8.
+        let passes = [(0, 0, 8, 8), (4, 0, 8, 8), (0, 4, 4, 8),
+                      (2, 0, 4, 4), (0, 2, 2, 4), (1, 0, 2, 2), (0, 1, 1, 2)]
+        var raw = Data()
+        for (x0, y0, dx, dy) in passes {
+            for _ in stride(from: y0, to: 8, by: dy) {
+                raw.append(0)
+                for _ in stride(from: x0, to: 8, by: dx) { raw.append(contentsOf: pixel) }
+            }
+        }
+        return raw // 64 pixels×4 + 15 filter bytes = 271 bytes
+    }
+
+    static func png(idats: [Data], interlace: UInt8 = 0) -> Data {
+        var bytes = Data([137, 80, 78, 71, 13, 10, 26, 10])
+        var header = bigEndian32(8)
+        header.append(bigEndian32(8))
+        header.append(contentsOf: [8, 6, 0, 0, interlace]) // 8-bit RGBA
+        bytes.append(chunk("IHDR", header))
+        bytes.append(chunk("sRGB", Data([0]))) // explicit sRGB, perceptual intent
+        for payload in idats { bytes.append(chunk("IDAT", payload)) }
+        bytes.append(chunk("IEND", Data()))
+        return bytes
+    }
+
+    static func zlibStored(_ raw: Data) -> Data {
+        precondition(raw.count <= 65_535)
+        let length = UInt16(raw.count)
+        let complement = ~length
+        var bytes = Data([0x78, 0x01, 0x01, // zlib CMF/FLG, BFINAL=1/BTYPE=00
+                          UInt8(truncatingIfNeeded: length), UInt8(length >> 8),
+                          UInt8(truncatingIfNeeded: complement), UInt8(complement >> 8)])
+        bytes.append(raw)
+        var a: UInt32 = 1
+        var b: UInt32 = 0
+        for byte in raw {
+            a = (a + UInt32(byte)) % 65_521
+            b = (b + a) % 65_521
+        }
+        bytes.append(bigEndian32((b << 16) | a))
+        return bytes
+    }
+
+    private static func chunk(_ name: String, _ payload: Data) -> Data {
+        var crcInput = Data(name.utf8)
+        crcInput.append(payload)
+        var crc: UInt32 = 0xffff_ffff
+        for byte in crcInput {
+            crc ^= UInt32(byte)
+            for _ in 0..<8 { crc = (crc >> 1) ^ ((crc & 1) == 1 ? 0xedb8_8320 : 0) }
+        }
+        var bytes = bigEndian32(UInt32(payload.count))
+        bytes.append(crcInput)
+        bytes.append(bigEndian32(crc ^ 0xffff_ffff))
+        return bytes
+    }
+
+    static func littleEndian32(_ value: UInt32) -> Data {
+        Data([UInt8(truncatingIfNeeded: value), UInt8(truncatingIfNeeded: value >> 8),
+              UInt8(truncatingIfNeeded: value >> 16), UInt8(truncatingIfNeeded: value >> 24)])
+    }
+
+    private static func bigEndian32(_ value: UInt32) -> Data {
+        Data([UInt8(truncatingIfNeeded: value >> 24), UInt8(truncatingIfNeeded: value >> 16),
+              UInt8(truncatingIfNeeded: value >> 8), UInt8(truncatingIfNeeded: value)])
+    }
+}
diff --git a/ios/FocusMateTests/CardPhotoMemoryTests.swift b/ios/FocusMateTests/CardPhotoMemoryTests.swift
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..68e4f78
--- /dev/null
+++ b/ios/FocusMateTests/CardPhotoMemoryTests.swift
@@ -0,0 +1,298 @@
+import Foundation
+import CoreGraphics
+import Darwin
+import Dispatch
+import ImageIO
+import XCTest
+@testable import FocusMate
+
+final class CardPhotoMemoryTests: XCTestCase {
+    // Diagnostic reference only: measure ImageIO's own WebP thumbnail decoder
+    // separately in a fresh test process before the production regression test.
+    func testI1_ImageIOThumbnailOnly8192_memoryDiagnostic() throws {
+        let input = try fixture(Self.webp8192)
+        let sampler = try MemorySampler()
+        let thumbnail: CGImage
+        let measurement: MemoryMeasurement
+        do {
+            thumbnail = try autoreleasepool {
+                let source = try XCTUnwrap(CGImageSourceCreateWithData(input as CFData,
+                    [kCGImageSourceShouldCache: false] as CFDictionary))
+                return try XCTUnwrap(CGImageSourceCreateThumbnailAtIndex(source, 0, [
+                    kCGImageSourceCreateThumbnailFromImageAlways: true,
+                    kCGImageSourceThumbnailMaxPixelSize: 2048,
+                    kCGImageSourceCreateThumbnailWithTransform: true,
+                    kCGImageSourceShouldCache: true,
+                    kCGImageSourceShouldCacheImmediately: true
+                ] as CFDictionary))
+            }
+            measurement = try sampler.finish()
+        } catch {
+            sampler.cancelAndWait()
+            throw error
+        }
+        print(measurement.description(label: "ImageIO-thumbnail-only-8192"))
+        assertReliable(measurement)
+        XCTAssertEqual(thumbnail.width, 2048)
+        XCTAssertEqual(thumbnail.height, 2048)
+        // Small draw forces observable valid pixels AFTER memory measurement.
+        assertSourceColor(try sampledRGBA(thumbnail))
+    }
+
+    // 깨짐 명명: bounded thumbnail 이후 원본 전체를 추가 decode하면 정상 압축
+    // 입력에서도 RSS growth가 증가한다. Fixture/environment-specific 384MiB budget:
+    // fresh iOS 26.5 thumbnail-only measured ~286MiB, plus ~98MiB for bounded
+    // 2048 output, a 16MiB context, and encoding/readback caches. This is not
+    // a global memory limit for all image formats. Set before production RED execution.
+    func testI1_FirstProduction8192_preservesImageWithoutRedundantOriginalDecodeWithin384MiB() async throws {
+        let input = try fixture(Self.webp8192)
+        // No large-original decode and no processor warmup before this first call.
+        let (result, measurement) = try await measuredProcess(input)
+        print(measurement.description(label: "production-first-8192-384MiB"))
+        assertReliable(measurement)
+        XCTAssertLessThanOrEqual(measurement.sampledRSSGrowth, 402653184,
+            "8192 WebP fixture on the measured environment must stay within 384MiB RSS growth without redundant original decode; this is not an all-formats limit.")
+        try assertOutput(result)
+    }
+
+    // Diagnostic success at a smaller input size; no memory-bound assertion.
+    // 깨짐 명명: empty output / wrong dimensions / replaced color fails this case.
+    func testI1_FirstProduction4096_memoryDiagnosticAndSuccessfulOutput() async throws {
+        let input = try fixture(Self.webp4096)
+        let (result, measurement) = try await measuredProcess(input)
+        print(measurement.description(label: "production-first-4096-diagnostic"))
+        assertReliable(measurement)
+        try assertOutput(result)
+    }
+
+    private func measuredProcess(_ input: Data) async throws -> (ProcessedCardPhoto, MemoryMeasurement) {
+        let processor = ImageIOCardPhotoProcessor()
+        let sampler = try MemorySampler()
+        do {
+            let result = try await processor.process(input)
+            let measurement = try sampler.finish()
+            return (result, measurement)
+        } catch {
+            sampler.cancelAndWait()
+            throw error
+        }
+    }
+
+    private func assertReliable(_ measurement: MemoryMeasurement,
+                                file: StaticString = #filePath, line: UInt = #line) {
+        XCTAssertGreaterThan(measurement.timerSampleCount, 0,
+            "No timer samples: memory evidence is insufficient", file: file, line: line)
+        XCTAssertTrue(measurement.errors.isEmpty,
+            "TASK_VM_INFO sampling errors: \(measurement.errors)", file: file, line: line)
+    }
+
+    private func assertOutput(_ result: ProcessedCardPhoto) throws {
+        XCTAssertEqual(result.pixelWidth, 2048)
+        XCTAssertEqual(result.pixelHeight, 2048)
+        XCTAssertFalse(result.data.isEmpty)
+        XCTAssertLessThanOrEqual(result.data.count, 1_048_576)
+        // Decode only the small output, after the sampler has stopped and joined.
+        let source = try XCTUnwrap(CGImageSourceCreateWithData(result.data as CFData, nil))
+        let image = try XCTUnwrap(CGImageSourceCreateImageAtIndex(source, 0,
+            [kCGImageSourceShouldCacheImmediately: true] as CFDictionary))
+        XCTAssertEqual(image.width, 2048)
+        XCTAssertEqual(image.height, 2048)
+        assertSourceColor(try sampledRGBA(image))
+    }
+
+    private func sampledRGBA(_ image: CGImage) throws -> [UInt8] {
+        var bytes = [UInt8](repeating: 0, count: 4)
+        try bytes.withUnsafeMutableBytes { buffer in
+            let context = try XCTUnwrap(CGContext(data: buffer.baseAddress, width: 1, height: 1,
+                bitsPerComponent: 8, bytesPerRow: 4,
+                space: try XCTUnwrap(CGColorSpace(name: CGColorSpace.sRGB)),
+                bitmapInfo: CGBitmapInfo.byteOrder32Big.rawValue |
+                    CGImageAlphaInfo.premultipliedLast.rawValue))
+            context.draw(image, in: CGRect(x: 0, y: 0, width: 1, height: 1))
+        }
+        return bytes
+    }
+
+    private func assertSourceColor(_ rgba: [UInt8]) {
+        XCTAssertEqual(Double(rgba[0]), 220, accuracy: 8)
+        XCTAssertEqual(Double(rgba[1]), 40, accuracy: 8)
+        XCTAssertEqual(Double(rgba[2]), 80, accuracy: 8)
+        XCTAssertEqual(rgba[3], 255)
+    }

+    private func fixture(_ base64: String) throws -> Data {
+        try XCTUnwrap(Data(base64Encoded: base64, options: .ignoreUnknownCharacters))
+    }

+private struct MemorySnapshot: Sendable {
+    let rss: UInt64
+    let physicalFootprint: UInt64
+    let kernelLifetimePeakRSS: UInt64
+
+    static func read() throws -> MemorySnapshot {
+        var info = task_vm_info_data_t()
+        let capacity = MemoryLayout<task_vm_info_data_t>.size / MemoryLayout<integer_t>.size
+        var count = mach_msg_type_number_t(capacity)
+        let status = withUnsafeMutablePointer(to: &info) { pointer in
+            pointer.withMemoryRebound(to: integer_t.self, capacity: capacity) {
+                task_info(mach_task_self_, task_flavor_t(TASK_VM_INFO), $0, &count)
+            }
+        }
+        guard status == KERN_SUCCESS else { throw MemoryProbeError.taskInfo(status) }
+        // REV1 contains physical footprint. Never silently read absent fields as 0.
+        let footprintEnd = MemoryLayout<task_vm_info_data_t>.offset(of: \.phys_footprint)! +
+            MemoryLayout.size(ofValue: info.phys_footprint)
+        guard Int(count) * MemoryLayout<integer_t>.size >= footprintEnd else {
+            throw MemoryProbeError.shortTaskInfo(count)
+        }
+        return MemorySnapshot(rss: UInt64(info.resident_size),
+            physicalFootprint: UInt64(info.phys_footprint),
+            kernelLifetimePeakRSS: UInt64(info.resident_size_peak))
+    }
+}
+
+private enum MemoryProbeError: Error {
+    case taskInfo(kern_return_t)
+    case shortTaskInfo(mach_msg_type_number_t)
+}
+
+private struct MemoryMeasurement: Sendable {
+    let before: MemorySnapshot
+    let after: MemorySnapshot
+    let maximumSampleRSS: UInt64
+    let timerSampleCount: Int
+    let errors: [String]
+
+    var sampledRSSGrowth: UInt64 {
+        maximumSampleRSS >= before.rss ? maximumSampleRSS - before.rss : 0
+    }
+
+    func description(label: String) -> String {
+        "MEMORY_PROBE \(label) baselineRSS=\(before.rss) maxSampleRSS=\(maximumSampleRSS) " +
+        "sampledRSSGrowth=\(sampledRSSGrowth) afterRSS=\(after.rss) " +
+        "physFootprintBefore=\(before.physicalFootprint) physFootprintAfter=\(after.physicalFootprint) " +
+        "kernelLifetimePeakRSSBefore=\(before.kernelLifetimePeakRSS) " +
+        "kernelLifetimePeakRSSAfter=\(after.kernelLifetimePeakRSS) " +
+        "timerSampleCount=\(timerSampleCount) errors=\(errors) " +
+        "sampledPeakIsLowerBound=true kernelPeakIsNotResettable=true"
+    }
+}
+
+// @unchecked Sendable is confined to this recorder: all mutable sample state is
+// protected by NSLock. The serial queue orders callbacks; cancellation is joined
+// before reading a report or releasing the timer's strong capture of self.
+private final class MemorySampler: @unchecked Sendable {
+    private let lock = NSLock()
+    private let queue = DispatchQueue(label: "FocusMateTests.CardPhotoMemorySampler", qos: .userInitiated)
+    private let cancellation = DispatchGroup()
+    private let timer: DispatchSourceTimer
+    private let before: MemorySnapshot
+    private var maximumSampleRSS: UInt64
+    private var timerSampleCount = 0
+    private var errors: [String] = []
+
+    init() throws {
+        before = try MemorySnapshot.read()
+        maximumSampleRSS = before.rss
+        timer = DispatchSource.makeTimerSource(queue: queue)
+        cancellation.enter()
+        let completion = cancellation
+        timer.setCancelHandler { completion.leave() }
+        timer.setEventHandler { [self] in sample() }
+        timer.schedule(deadline: .now(), repeating: .milliseconds(1), leeway: .nanoseconds(0))
+        timer.resume()
+    }
+
+    private func sample() {
+        do {
+            let snapshot = try MemorySnapshot.read()
+            lock.lock()
+            maximumSampleRSS = max(maximumSampleRSS, snapshot.rss)
+            timerSampleCount += 1
+            lock.unlock()
+        } catch {
+            lock.lock()
+            errors.append(String(describing: error))
+            lock.unlock()
+        }
+    }
+
+    // Only the test's measuring caller invokes lifecycle methods, never the
+    // timer queue. Cancel + group wait drains any in-flight callback.
+    func cancelAndWait() {
+        timer.cancel()
+        cancellation.wait()
+        timer.setEventHandler {}
+        timer.setCancelHandler {}
+    }
+
+    func finish() throws -> MemoryMeasurement {
+        let after: MemorySnapshot
+        do {
+            after = try MemorySnapshot.read()
+        } catch {
+            cancelAndWait()
+            throw error
+        }
+        cancelAndWait()
+        lock.lock()
+        defer { lock.unlock() }
+        return MemoryMeasurement(before: before, after: after,
+            maximumSampleRSS: max(maximumSampleRSS, after.rss),
+            timerSampleCount: timerSampleCount, errors: errors)
+    }
+}

+import Foundation
+import ImageIO
+import UniformTypeIdentifiers
+import XCTest
+@testable import FocusMate
+
+final class CardPhotoProcessorTests: XCTestCase {
+    private func assertProcessIssue(_ expected: CardPhotoProcessingIssue, input: Data,
+                                    file: StaticString = #filePath, line: UInt = #line) async {
+        do {
+            _ = try await ImageIOCardPhotoProcessor().process(input)
+            XCTFail("Expected \(expected)", file: file, line: line)
+        } catch {
+            XCTAssertEqual(error as? CardPhotoProcessingIssue, expected, file: file, line: line)
+        }
+    }
+    private func assertStoredInvalid(_ photo: CardPhoto,
+                                     file: StaticString = #filePath, line: UInt = #line) {
+        XCTAssertThrowsError(try ImageIOCardPhotoProcessor.validateStoredPhoto(photo), file: file, line: line) {
+            XCTAssertEqual($0 as? SnapshotValidationIssue, .invalidCardPhoto, file: file, line: line)
+        }
+    }
+
+    // 깨짐 명명: 빈 반환 또는 디코딩하지 않은 입력 반환은 실패한다.
+    func testAC05_JPEG_실제이미지는_보존가능한_내용으로_처리된다() async throws {
+        let input = try CardPhotoTestFixtures.image(type: .jpeg)
+        _ = try CardPhotoTestFixtures.completeDecoded(input)
+        // 원본 fixture sanity: processor와 독립적으로 sRGB 입력 의미를 확인한다.
+        let inputRGBA = try CardPhotoTestFixtures.rgba(input)
+        XCTAssertGreaterThan(inputRGBA[0], 240, "원본 fixture sRGB red")
+        XCTAssertLessThan(inputRGBA[1], 15, "원본 fixture sRGB green")
+        XCTAssertLessThan(inputRGBA[2], 15, "원본 fixture sRGB blue")
+        XCTAssertEqual(inputRGBA[3], 255, "원본 fixture alpha")
+        let result = try await ImageIOCardPhotoProcessor().process(input)
+        let decoded = try CardPhotoTestFixtures.decoded(result.data)
+        XCTAssertEqual(decoded.width, 40)
+        XCTAssertEqual(decoded.height, 20)
+        XCTAssertEqual(result.pixelWidth, 40)
+        XCTAssertEqual(result.pixelHeight, 20)
+        XCTAssertLessThanOrEqual(result.data.count, 1_048_576)
+        let rgba = try CardPhotoTestFixtures.rgba(result.data)
+        XCTAssertGreaterThan(rgba[0], 240)
+        XCTAssertLessThan(rgba[1], 15)
+        XCTAssertLessThan(rgba[2], 15)
+    }
+

+        XCTAssertNoThrow(try ImageIOCardPhotoProcessor.validateStoredPhoto(CardPhotoTestFixtures.photo(data: result.data, width: 2048, height: 1024)))
+    }
+
+    // 깨짐 명명: 저장 이미지 실제 decode·치수·긴 변·비율 확인을 생략하면 실패한다.
+    func testDeclaredWidthMismatch() throws {
+        assertStoredInvalid(CardPhotoTestFixtures.photo(data: try CardPhotoTestFixtures.image(), width: 41, height: 20))
+    }
+
+    // 깨짐 명명: 저장 이미지 실제 decode·치수·긴 변·비율 확인을 생략하면 실패한다.
+    func testDeclaredHeightMismatch() throws {
+        assertStoredInvalid(CardPhotoTestFixtures.photo(data: try CardPhotoTestFixtures.image(), width: 40, height: 21))
+    }
+
+    // 깨짐 명명: 저장 이미지 실제 decode·치수·긴 변·비율 확인을 생략하면 실패한다.
+    func testStoredLongSideTooLarge() throws {
+        assertStoredInvalid(CardPhotoTestFixtures.photo(data: try CardPhotoTestFixtures.image(width: 2049, height: 1025), width: 2049, height: 1025))
+    }
+
+    // 깨짐 명명: 저장 이미지 실제 decode·치수·긴 변·비율 확인을 생략하면 실패한다.
+    func testStoredRatioBelow() throws {
+        assertStoredInvalid(CardPhotoTestFixtures.photo(data: try CardPhotoTestFixtures.image(width: 9, height: 100), width: 9, height: 100))
+    }
+
+    // 깨짐 명명: 저장 이미지 실제 decode·치수·긴 변·비율 확인을 생략하면 실패한다.
+    func testStoredRatioAbove() throws {
+        assertStoredInvalid(CardPhotoTestFixtures.photo(data: try CardPhotoTestFixtures.image(width: 101, height: 10), width: 101, height: 10))
+    }
+
+    // 깨짐 명명: 저장 이미지 실제 decode·치수·긴 변·비율 확인을 생략하면 실패한다.
+    func testStoredDamagedBody() throws {
+        assertStoredInvalid(CardPhotoTestFixtures.photo(data: try CardPhotoTestFixtures.damagedBody(), width: 40, height: 20))
+    }
+
+    // 깨짐 명명: 저장 이미지 실제 decode·치수·긴 변·비율 확인을 생략하면 실패한다.
+    func testStoredEmpty() throws {
+        assertStoredInvalid(CardPhotoTestFixtures.photo(data: Data(), width: 40, height: 20))
+    }
+
+    // 깨짐 명명: 저장 이미지 실제 decode·치수·긴 변·비율 확인을 생략하면 실패한다.
+    func testStoredUnsupported() throws {
+        assertStoredInvalid(CardPhotoTestFixtures.photo(data: Data("unsupported".utf8), width: 40, height: 20))
+    }
+
+    // 깨짐 명명: 읽을 수 있는 WebP를 거절하거나 빈/다른 색 이미지로 반환하면 실패한다.
+    func testAC05_WebP_실제이미지는_보존가능한_내용으로_처리된다() async throws {
+        let input = try CardPhotoTestFixtures.losslessWebP()
+        let inputImage = try CardPhotoTestFixtures.completeDecoded(input)
+        XCTAssertEqual(inputImage.width, 40)
+        XCTAssertEqual(inputImage.height, 20)
+        let result = try await ImageIOCardPhotoProcessor().process(input)
+        let decoded = try CardPhotoTestFixtures.completeDecoded(result.data)
+        XCTAssertEqual(decoded.width, 40)
+        XCTAssertEqual(decoded.height, 20)
+        XCTAssertEqual(Double(decoded.width) / Double(decoded.height), 2)
+        XCTAssertEqual(result.pixelWidth, 40)
+        XCTAssertEqual(result.pixelHeight, 20)
+        XCTAssertLessThanOrEqual(result.data.count, 1_048_576)
+        let rgba = try CardPhotoTestFixtures.rgba(result.data)
+        // 불투명 입력. JPEG 재인코딩 오차는 허용하되 다른 색으로 대체하면 실패한다.
+        XCTAssertEqual(Double(rgba[0]), 220, accuracy: 8)
+        XCTAssertEqual(Double(rgba[1]), 40, accuracy: 8)
+        XCTAssertEqual(Double(rgba[2]), 80, accuracy: 8)
+        XCTAssertEqual(rgba[3], 255)
+        XCTAssertNoThrow(try ImageIOCardPhotoProcessor.validateStoredPhoto(
+            CardPhotoTestFixtures.photo(data: result.data, width: 40, height: 20)))
+    }
+
+    // 깨짐 명명: 입력 한도를 >=8MiB로 거절하거나 메타데이터 포함 원본을 그대로 반환하면 실패한다.
+    func testAC05_Exact8MiB_완전한PNG는_내용을_보존하여_처리된다() async throws {
+        let input = try CardPhotoTestFixtures.exact8MiBPNG()
+        XCTAssertEqual(input.count, 8_388_608)
+        let inputImage = try CardPhotoTestFixtures.completeDecoded(input)
+        XCTAssertEqual(inputImage.width, 40)
+        XCTAssertEqual(inputImage.height, 20)
+        // 원본 fixture sanity: processor와 독립적으로 sRGB 입력 의미를 확인한다.
+        let inputRGBA = try CardPhotoTestFixtures.rgba(input)
+        XCTAssertGreaterThan(inputRGBA[0], 240, "원본 fixture sRGB red")
+        XCTAssertLessThan(inputRGBA[1], 15, "원본 fixture sRGB green")
+        XCTAssertLessThan(inputRGBA[2], 15, "원본 fixture sRGB blue")
+        XCTAssertEqual(inputRGBA[3], 255, "원본 fixture alpha")
+        let result = try await ImageIOCardPhotoProcessor().process(input)
+        let decoded = try CardPhotoTestFixtures.completeDecoded(result.data)
+        XCTAssertEqual(decoded.width, 40)
+        XCTAssertEqual(decoded.height, 20)
+        XCTAssertEqual(Double(decoded.width) / Double(decoded.height), 2)
+        XCTAssertEqual(result.pixelWidth, 40)
+        XCTAssertEqual(result.pixelHeight, 20)
+        XCTAssertLessThanOrEqual(result.data.count, 1_048_576)
+        let rgba = try CardPhotoTestFixtures.rgba(result.data)
+        XCTAssertGreaterThan(rgba[0], 240)
+        XCTAssertLessThan(rgba[1], 15)
+        XCTAssertLessThan(rgba[2], 15)
+        XCTAssertEqual(rgba[3], 255)
+        XCTAssertNoThrow(try ImageIOCardPhotoProcessor.validateStoredPhoto(
+            CardPhotoTestFixtures.photo(data: result.data, width: 40, height: 20)))
+    }

+import Foundation
+import CoreGraphics
+import ImageIO
+import UniformTypeIdentifiers
+import XCTest
+@testable import FocusMate
+
+/// 실제 ImageIO 입력. 프로덕션 processor/encoder로 기대 이미지를 만들지 않는다.
+enum CardPhotoTestFixtures {
+    static let photoID = UUID(uuidString: "11111111-2222-3333-4444-555555555555")!
+
+    static func image(width: Int = 40, height: Int = 20, alpha: Bool = false,
+                      type: UTType = .png, orientation: Int = 1) throws -> Data {
+        let context = try XCTUnwrap(CGContext(data: nil, width: width, height: height,
+            bitsPerComponent: 8, bytesPerRow: width * 4, space: try XCTUnwrap(CGColorSpace(name: CGColorSpace.sRGB)),
+            bitmapInfo: CGImageAlphaInfo.premultipliedLast.rawValue))
+        // 입력의 pure red는 context와 같은 명시적 sRGB에서 정의한다.
+        let color = try XCTUnwrap(CGColor(colorSpace: try XCTUnwrap(context.colorSpace),
+            components: [1, 0, 0, alpha ? 0.5 : 1]))
+        context.setFillColor(color)
+        context.fill(CGRect(x: 0, y: 0, width: CGFloat(width), height: CGFloat(height)))
+        let image = try XCTUnwrap(context.makeImage())
+        let bytes = NSMutableData()
+        let destination = try XCTUnwrap(CGImageDestinationCreateWithData(bytes, type.identifier as CFString, 1, nil))
+        CGImageDestinationAddImage(destination, image, [kCGImagePropertyOrientation: orientation] as CFDictionary)
+        guard CGImageDestinationFinalize(destination) else { throw FixtureIssue.encoding }
+        return bytes as Data
+    }
+
+    static func source(_ bytes: Data) throws -> CGImageSource {
+        try XCTUnwrap(CGImageSourceCreateWithData(bytes as CFData, nil))
+    }
+
+    static func decoded(_ bytes: Data) throws -> CGImage {
+        let source = try source(bytes)
+        return try XCTUnwrap(CGImageSourceCreateImageAtIndex(source, 0, nil))
+    }
+
+    static func rgba(_ bytes: Data) throws -> [UInt8] {
+        let image = try decoded(bytes)
+        let context = try XCTUnwrap(CGContext(data: nil, width: 1, height: 1,
+            bitsPerComponent: 8, bytesPerRow: 4, space: try XCTUnwrap(CGColorSpace(name: CGColorSpace.sRGB)),
+            bitmapInfo: CGImageAlphaInfo.premultipliedLast.rawValue))
+        context.draw(image, in: CGRect(x: 0, y: 0, width: 1, height: 1))
+        let pointer = try XCTUnwrap(context.data).assumingMemoryBound(to: UInt8.self)
+        return Array(UnsafeBufferPointer(start: pointer, count: 4))
+    }
+
+    /// PNG signature와 IHDR은 남기고 IDAT payload를 깨뜨린다. 헤더만 읽는 구현을 잡는다.
+    static func damagedBody() throws -> Data {
+        var bytes = try image()
+        let range = try XCTUnwrap(bytes.range(of: Data("IDAT".utf8)))
+        let start = range.upperBound
+        guard start + 4 < bytes.count else { throw FixtureIssue.encoding }
+        bytes.replaceSubrange(start..<(bytes.count - 12), with: Data(repeating: 0, count: bytes.count - 12 - start))
+        return bytes
+    }
+
+    static func photo(data: Data = Data([1]), width: Int = 40, height: Int = 20,
+                      id: UUID = photoID, x: Double = 0.5, y: Double = 0.5,
+                      fraction: Double = 0.6) -> CardPhoto {
+        CardPhoto(id: id, data: data, pixelWidth: width, pixelHeight: height,
+                  centerX: x, centerY: y, widthFraction: fraction)
+    }
+
+    static func style(_ photos: [CardPhoto]) -> CardStyle {
+        CardStyle(background: CardColor(red: 0.2, green: 0.3, blue: 0.4), photos: photos)
+    }
+
+    static func seed(_ bytes: Data, at url: URL) throws {
+        try FileManager.default.createDirectory(at: url.deletingLastPathComponent(), withIntermediateDirectories: true)
+        try bytes.write(to: url)
+    }
+
+
+    /// 부모가 번들 Python3 Pillow lossless WebP encoder로 자체 생성한 40×20 RGB(220,40,80), 38bytes.
+    /// 외부 사진/라이선스 없음. 출처: .dev/codex-feat-card-customization/reports/t2-fixture-input.md,
+    /// test-webp-base64.txt 및 t2-fixture-validation.log (macOS ImageIO complete/decode/draw 성공).
+    /// 대상 iOS 지원은 skip 없이 실제 processor 테스트에서 검증한다.
+    static func losslessWebP() throws -> Data {
+        try XCTUnwrap(Data(base64Encoded: "UklGRh4AAABXRUJQVlA4TBEAAAAvJ8AEAAdQlHIXqv+BiOh/AAA="))
+    }
+
+    /// 기존 ImageIO PNG의 IEND 앞에 정상 tEXt 메타데이터를 삽입한다. 후행 padding이 아니다.
+    /// 전체 입력 한도에는 메타데이터도 포함된다: base + 12(chunk overhead) + 8(key+NUL) + payload.
+    static func exact8MiBPNG() throws -> Data {
+        var bytes = try image()
+        let targetCount = 8_388_608
+        let keyword = Data("padding\0".utf8)
+        let payloadCount = targetCount - bytes.count - 12 - keyword.count
+        guard payloadCount >= 0 else { throw FixtureIssue.encoding }
+
+        // PNG chunk 경계를 따라 IEND를 찾는다. 압축 본문 안의 우연한 문자열과 혼동하지 않는다.
+        var offset = 8
+        var iendOffset: Int?
+        while offset <= bytes.count - 12 {
+            let length = bytes[offset..<(offset + 4)].reduce(0) { ($0 << 8) | Int($1) }
+            guard length <= bytes.count - offset - 12 else { throw FixtureIssue.encoding }
+            let type = bytes[(offset + 4)..<(offset + 8)]
+            if Data(type) == Data("IEND".utf8) {
+                guard length == 0, offset + 12 == bytes.count else { throw FixtureIssue.encoding }
+                iendOffset = offset
+                break
+            }
+            offset += length + 12
+        }
+        let insertionOffset = try XCTUnwrap(iendOffset)
+        var text = keyword
+        text.append(Data(repeating: 0x78, count: payloadCount)) // 유효한 Latin-1 tEXt 값 'x'.
+        var crcInput = Data("tEXt".utf8)
+        crcInput.append(text)
+        var chunk = bigEndianBytes(UInt32(text.count))
+        chunk.append(crcInput)
+        chunk.append(bigEndianBytes(crc32(crcInput)))
+        bytes.insert(contentsOf: chunk, at: insertionOffset)
+        return bytes
+    }
+
+    private static func bigEndianBytes(_ value: UInt32) -> Data {
+        Data([UInt8(truncatingIfNeeded: value >> 24), UInt8(truncatingIfNeeded: value >> 16),
+              UInt8(truncatingIfNeeded: value >> 8), UInt8(truncatingIfNeeded: value)])
+    }
+
+    // PNG CRC는 chunk type+data를 대상으로 하는 표준 reflected CRC32다.
+    private static func crc32(_ bytes: Data) -> UInt32 {
+        var crc: UInt32 = 0xFFFF_FFFF
+        for byte in bytes {
+            crc ^= UInt32(byte)
+            for _ in 0..<8 {
+                crc = (crc >> 1) ^ ((crc & 1) == 1 ? 0xEDB8_8320 : 0)
+            }
+        }
+        return crc ^ 0xFFFF_FFFF
+    }
+
+    /// 프로덕션 processor와 독립적인 ImageIO 검사. 부분 복구 입력도 성공으로 인정하지 않는다.
+    static func completeDecoded(_ bytes: Data) throws -> CGImage {
+        let source = try source(bytes)
+        guard CGImageSourceGetStatus(source) == .statusComplete,
+              CGImageSourceGetStatusAtIndex(source, 0) == .statusComplete else {
+            XCTFail("Fixture/output ImageIO source and index must be complete")
+            throw FixtureIssue.encoding
+        }
+        let image = try XCTUnwrap(CGImageSourceCreateImageAtIndex(source, 0, nil))
+        let context = try XCTUnwrap(CGContext(data: nil, width: image.width, height: image.height,
+            bitsPerComponent: 8, bytesPerRow: image.width * 4, space: try XCTUnwrap(CGColorSpace(name: CGColorSpace.sRGB)),
+            bitmapInfo: CGImageAlphaInfo.premultipliedLast.rawValue))
+        context.draw(image, in: CGRect(x: 0, y: 0, width: CGFloat(image.width), height: CGFloat(image.height)))
+        _ = try XCTUnwrap(context.data)
+        return image
+    }
+
+    enum FixtureIssue: Error { case encoding }
+}
